### [1] [Vision Foundation Models with Synthetic-Only Training for Monocular Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26561)
*John Church,Vazghen Nikolian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在已知非合作航天器位姿估计任务中，用DINOv3大型自监督ViT替换原有小型卷积/ViT编码器，并结合LoRA、多种子集成与四旋转测试时增强，在SPEED+ lightbox和sunlamp测试集上取得更低旋转误差，同时验证了Jetson Orin NX上的嵌入式推理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有航天器位姿估计架构精度有限，且需在具备轨道飞行 heritage 的嵌入式处理器上可部署；目标是在仅用合成数据训练的条件下，提升已知非合作航天器在lightbox与sunlamp域上的位姿估计精度，并评估嵌入式推理可行性。

Method: 基于既有heatmap位姿估计架构，将编码器替换为大型自监督ViT基础模型DINOv3，并采用LoRA微调（rank 64）；最佳模型使用840M参数、三种子集成与四旋转测试时增强；在SPEED+ lightbox和sunlamp测试集评估旋转误差，并在Jetson Orin NX 16GB上测量单次网络推理时间与功耗。

Result: 模型规模从300M提升至840M且未观察到饱和；840M模型在Jetson Orin NX 16GB上单次裁剪推理为133.8 ms，板卡功耗32.0 W；最佳模型在sunlamp上平均旋转误差为1.56°，在lightbox上为1.17°，优于此前已知最佳EagerNet的2.66°和1.75°。

Conclusion: 将DINOv3基础模型适配到heatmap位姿估计流程可显著提升非合作航天器位姿估计精度，且840M模型仍可在具轨道飞行 heritage 的嵌入式处理器上运行，表明大规模自监督ViT结合LoRA在航天器位姿估计中兼顾精度与嵌入式部署潜力。

Abstract: We present an improvement on previous spacecraft pose estimation architectures that results in the lowest published mean rotation errors we know of on the SPEED+ lightbox and sunlamp test sets for a known, non-cooperative spacecraft. By using a previously established heatmap-based pose estimation architecture and adapting a large self-supervised ViT foundation model (DINOv3) in place of the smaller convolutional and ViT encoders of previous work, we show that pose estimation accuracy improves from 300M to 840M parameters with no saturation yet observed. We also evaluate our 840M model on a Jetson Orin NX 16GB, measuring single-pass network inference at 133.8 ms per crop with a board draw of 32.0 W. These measurements demonstrate embedded inference feasibility on a processor family with orbital flight heritage. Our resulting model outperforms previous models across lightbox and sunlamp domains while training only on synthetic data. Our best model, using DINOv3 840M adapted with LoRA as the encoder (rank 64, three-seed ensemble with four-rotation test-time augmentation), results in $1.56^\circ$ mean rotation error on sunlamp and $1.17^\circ$ on lightbox, compared to the previous best mean rotation errors we know of on these test sets, $2.66^\circ$ and $1.75^\circ$ by EagerNet.

</details>


### [2] [Leveraging Vision-Based Point Cloud Map Priors for Camera-Based 3D Object Detection and Online Vectorized HD Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26325)
*Markus Käppeler,Rohit Mohan,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需LiDAR的视觉先验框架：利用历史相机轨迹通过Pi3X构建静态点云先验地图，并融合DINOv3语义特征；运行时经全局定位检索局部先验，稀疏体素编码后在BEV中与多视角图像特征融合，由任务特定稀疏Transformer头完成3D目标检测和矢量化HD地图构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机的3D目标检测与在线矢量化HD地图构建能提供紧凑场景表示，但依赖精确度量几何且受深度歧义限制。长期部署中，重复轨迹观测可累积为持久点云先验，提供当前观测之外的几何上下文。现有显式点云先验方法依赖LiDAR建图，需要昂贵3D测距传感器。

Method: 从先前相机轨迹使用Pi3X构建静态点云先验地图，并为每个点附加DINOv3特征。运行时利用全局定位检索局部先验块，通过稀疏体素主干编码，并在鸟瞰图（BEV）中与提升的多视角相机特征融合。随后使用任务特定稀疏Transformer头，从融合表示中预测3D目标和矢量化地图元素。

Result: 在Argoverse 2上，视觉先验将强基线从0.287提升至0.299 CDS，并将矢量化建图mAP从0.669提升至0.750。消融实验表明，语义DINOv3特征对矢量化建图尤为重要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，视觉构建的几何-语义先验可作为有效的长期场景记忆，用于基于相机的感知，在无需LiDAR进行先验地图构建或在线推理的情况下同时提升两项任务。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D object detection and online vectorized HD mapping provide compact scene representations for autonomous driving, but both depend on accurate metric geometry and remain limited by depth ambiguity. Over long-term deployment, observations from repeated traversals can be accumulated into persistent point cloud priors that provide geometric context beyond the current observations. Existing explicit point cloud prior approaches, however, rely on LiDAR-based map construction and therefore require expensive 3D ranging sensors. We propose a framework that constructs a static point cloud prior map from previous camera traversals using Pi3X and augments each point with DINOv3 features. At runtime, a local prior patch is retrieved using global localization, encoded with a sparse voxel backbone, and fused in bird's-eye view (BEV) with lifted multi-view camera features. Task-specific sparse transformer heads then predict 3D objects and vectorized map elements from the fused representation. On Argoverse 2, the vision-based prior improves a strong baseline from 0.287 to 0.299 CDS and from 0.669 to 0.750 vectorized mapping mAP. Ablations show that semantic DINOv3 features are particularly important for vectorized mapping. These results demonstrate that vision-built geometric-semantic priors provide an effective form of long-term scene memory for camera-based perception, improving both tasks without LiDAR for prior-map construction or online inference.

</details>


### [3] [MatchFusion: Explicit-Implicit Instance Matching for Spatio-Temporal Multimodal Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25860)
*Xiaoyu Li,Jiajia Fu,Long Shi,Tianyu Du,Ruihang Li,Xian Wu,Lijun Zhao,Yingtao Zhang,Lining Sun,Ruifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MatchFusion，一个可学习的实例匹配与融合模块，用于时空多模态自动驾驶，通过显式-隐式匹配高效实现LiDAR-相机及历史-当前实例交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏实例表示可为多模态感知与E2EAD提供紧凑的空间LiDAR-相机和时间历史-当前交互接口，但可靠实例对应需克服几何差异与异构语义表示。注意力方法能利用上下文语义，却常需专门表示对齐并增加计算开销；基于结构化对象状态的关联虽高效可解释，但缺乏上下文证据以解决模糊匹配。

Method: MatchFusion先用几何相似度和类别一致性初始化成对亲和力，再利用实例嵌入选择性精化结构上合理的关联；所得软匹配图指导一个通用残差聚合算子进行自适应信息交换。该统一匹配-融合形式支持空间LiDAR-相机交互和时间历史-当前交互，分别采用多视图图像平面几何与运动补偿BEV几何作为结构先验。

Result: 在nuScenes上，MatchFusion在不同前端配置下均带来一致的感知增益。相比先前以实例为中心的融合方法，配备MatchFusion的系统感知精度更高，同时FLOPs降低55.3%、GPU内存占用降低39.3%，且匹配-融合模块仅占总感知延迟的3.7%。将时序MatchFusion集成到SparseDrive中，可在E2E框架内无需额外监督进一步提升感知性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式-隐式匹配是时空实例交互的一种有效且高效的机制。

Abstract: Sparse instance representations provide a compact interface for spatial LiDAR-camera and temporal past-current interaction in multimodal perception and E2EAD. Effective interaction requires reliable instance correspondences despite geometric discrepancies and heterogeneous semantic representations. Attention-based methods exploit contextual semantics but often require specialized representation alignment, increasing computational overhead. In contrast, association based on structured object states is efficient and interpretable but lacks contextual evidence to resolve ambiguous matches. To combine these complementary strengths, we propose MatchFusion, a learnable instance matching and fusion module for spatio-temporal multimodal autonomous driving. MatchFusion initializes pairwise affinities using geometric similarity and category consistency, then selectively refines structurally plausible associations using instance embeddings. The resulting soft matchmap guides a common residual aggregation operator for adaptive information exchange. This unified matching-fusion formulation supports spatial LiDAR-camera and temporal past-current interaction, using multi-view image-plane geometry and motion-compensated BEV geometry as the respective structural priors. Experiments on nuScenes demonstrate consistent perception gains across diverse front-end configurations. Compared with a prior instance-centric fusion method, the MatchFusion-equipped system achieves higher perception accuracy while reducing FLOPs by 55.3% and GPU memory usage by 39.3%, with the matching-fusion module accounting for only 3.7% of total perception latency. Integrating temporal MatchFusion into SparseDrive further improves perception within an E2E framework without additional supervision. These results establish explicit-implicit matching as an effective and efficient mechanism for spatio-temporal instance interaction.

</details>

### [4] [Higher-Order Approximation of Exit Functionals in Sampling-Based Stochastic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25257)
*Sashank Modali,Takashi Tanaka*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文将高阶退出时间强逼近方法引入基于采样的随机模型预测控制，以降低首次退出时间和失效指示函数估计中的离散化误差；针对非交换与交换动力学分别采用自适应1阶Milstein加Wiktorsson Lévy面积模拟、以及自适应1.5阶构造，并在机会约束路径积分控制中验证其优于Euler-Maruyama。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型随机MPC的安全评估常需数值估计退出泛函，首次退出时间和退出/失效指示的逼近误差是关键数值瓶颈，且离散化误差会直接影响控制器，因此需要更高阶的强逼近方法。

Method: 针对一般非交换动力学，使用自适应1阶Milstein离散化，并通过Wiktorsson方法模拟Lévy面积；针对交换动力学，采用自适应1.5阶构造以获得更强的退出时间收敛率。在退出时间律满足局部反集中条件下，证明退出时间的强逼近可传递为失效指示函数的强逼近。随后将方法用于机会约束路径积分控制，该框架通过退出事件给出安全的精确连续时间表示。

Result: 数值实验比较了两类方法在强退出时间误差、失效指示误差和闭环约束满足方面的表现，结果显示相对Euler-Maruyama离散化有改进，并可用于依赖更强强逼近的现有及未来技术。

Conclusion: 高阶强退出时间逼近能够有效提升安全评估中退出泛函的数值精度，并转化为失效指示和闭环约束满足的改善；在机会约束路径积分控制中具有应用价值。

Abstract: Safety evaluation in sampling-based stochastic model predictive control often requires numerical estimation of exit functionals. The approximation of first-exit times and exit indicators is therefore a key numerical bottleneck, and discretization error in these quantities directly affects the resulting controller. This paper studies how existing higher-order methods for strong approximation of exit times can be brought into safe control. Two cases are highlighted. For general noncommutative dynamics, an adaptive order-1 Milstein discretization is used together with Lévy-area simulation via Wiktorsson's method. For commutative dynamics, an adaptive order-1.5 construction achieves a stronger exit-time rate. Under a local anti-concentration condition on the exit-time law, we show that strong exit-time approximation transfers to strong approximation of the failure indicator. The methods are then studied in the context of chance-constrained path integral control, which provides an exact continuous-time representation of safety through exit events. Numerical experiments compare the two cases in terms of strong exit-time error, failure-indicator error, and closed-loop constraint satisfaction, showing improvement over Euler-Maruyama and thereby enabling existing and future techniques whose applicability depends on improved strong approximation.

</details>

### [5] [Towards Adaptive Interaction Strategies for Human Companion Robot via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25031)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的移动机器人人类伴随策略，使机器人能根据环境与人类关系动态调整跟踪位置，并结合MPPI与CBF控制器实现安全、舒适且灵活的伴随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类伴随方法通常将机器人限制在人类后方、前方或侧方等固定相对位置，难以适应动态工作空间，限制了机器人的灵活性与适应性。

Method: 定义交互空间以刻画人-机器人-环境关系，并将其作为DRL状态空间的一部分，使机器人能适应环境变化；采用DRL动态调整跟踪位置；设计集成模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制与控制屏障函数（CBF）的人-机器人伴随控制器，在跟随目标位置与朝向的同时避障，并提升社会接受度与安全性。

Result: 在室内外真实场景中评估并与其他研究对比：成功率至少提升24%，跟踪精度至少提升47%，人类舒适度提高；机器人能以最高1.7 m/s的速度灵活伴随行人，动态调整策略而不局限于固定位置，并尊重人类亲密空间，实现安全、舒适和有效避障。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够实现灵活的人类伴随，在动态环境中提升跟踪性能、成功率、舒适性与安全性，表明DRL与MPPI-CBF结合在HRI伴随任务中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: In the field of Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), achieving flexibility in human-accompanying within real-world environments holds great potential for various applications but also poses significant challenges. Traditional methods typically restrict robots to fixed positions relative to humans, such as tracking from behind, in front, or side-by-side, which limits robot adaptability in dynamic workspaces. This study introduces a novel human-companioning strategy that uses Reinforcement Learning (DRL) to enable mobile robots to dynamically adjust their tracking positions according to varying conditions. An interaction space is defined to capture the relationship between the human and the robot while considering the environment, which serves as the basis for state spaces in DRL to assist the robot in adapting to environmental changes. A human-robot companion controller is developed by integrating Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control with Control Barrier Functions (CBF), ensuring that the robot accurately follows the target's movement in both position and orientation while avoiding obstacles and enhancing social acceptance and safety. The proposed approach is evaluated in real-world scenarios, both indoors and outdoors, and compared with other studies. The results show that the proposed method improves the success rate and tracking accuracy by at least 24% and 47%, respectively, while enhancing human comfort. Experiments demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly accompany a person walking at speeds of up to 1.7 m/s, dynamically adjusting its strategy without being confined to a fixed position. Additionally, the robot respects the human's intimate space to ensure safety, comfort, and effective obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [6] [ϕ-RIE: From Photorealistic Reconstruction to Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26795)
*Runyi Yang,Deheng Zhang,Xiaoye Wang,Kanzhi Wu,Lei Sun,Ajad Chhatkuli,Kunyu Peng,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: φ-RIE 是一种高斯原生流水线，将 3DGS 重建场景中的选定物体转换为可移动的仿真器资产，并补全背景，从而实现可交互场景转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 虽能照片级重建场景，但表示本身不支持物理交互；机器人仿真需要物体级变化，即物体独立运动、发生接触并暴露被遮挡环境。难点在于物体外观可能与背景纠缠，且隐藏几何与被遮挡背景内容未被观测。

Method: 提出 φ-RIE：将资产构建与源移除耦合，由同一物体身份同时定义可移动资产及需移除/补全的场景内容。Scene Observation 为 Coupled Scene Construction 提供共享证据，生成配准资产与补全后的背景高斯，供 Interactive Environment 中仿真器驱动渲染；耦合在保留未编辑高斯的同时对齐视觉与物理状态。

Result: 在 50 个 ScanNet++ 场景上，基于证据的选择与配准重试在固定保留率下将 20 mm 匹配 F1 从 0.336 提升至 0.383；进一步实验验证了资产可执行性、相对单生成器基线的操作增益以及转换的视觉代价。

Conclusion: 结果表明 φ-RIE 能够实现交互式场景转换。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) can reconstruct a captured scene photorealistically, but the resulting representation does not by itself support physical interaction. Robot simulation instead requires object-level change, \textit{i.e.}, objects must move independently, make contact, and reveal previously occluded surroundings. This gap arises because object appearance may remain entangled with the background, while hidden object geometry and occluded background content may be unobserved. To address this challenge, we present \phi-RIE, a Gaussian-native pipeline that converts selected objects into movable simulator assets while preserving the remaining reconstruction. Our key observation is that asset construction and source removal should be coupled, \textit{i.e.}, one object identity should define the movable asset and the scene content to remove and complete. Accordingly, Scene Observation supplies shared evidence to Coupled Scene Construction, which creates registered assets and completed background Gaussians for simulator-driven rendering in an Interactive Environment. This coupling preserves unedited Gaussians while aligning visual and physical state. On 50 ScanNet++ scenes, evidence-based selection and registration retry increase matched F1 at 20\,mm from 0.336 to 0.383 at fixed retention. Further tests demonstrate asset executability, manipulation gains over a single-generator baseline, and the visual cost of conversion. Together, these results demonstrate that \name\ enables interactive scene conversion.

</details>


### [7] [DreamStream: Towards Policy-Oriented Generative Simulation for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26792)
*Ziyang Leng,Sicheng Mo,Seth Z. Zhao,Haoyuan Cai,Yu Zeng,Rowan McAllister,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生成式闭环模拟器 DreamStream，用基于模拟器的自回归视频模型实现面向策略的保真度，并引入 FDπ 指标与 Navhard-CL 基准，以更忠实评估端到端驾驶策略的闭环决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台存在 sim-to-real 视觉差距，会破坏策略感知，无法忠实评估端到端驾驶策略的闭环决策；因此需要的不只是照片级真实，而是保留策略决策所依赖场景特征的观测。

Method: DreamStream 通过交通布局引导，从大型预训练视频模型蒸馏出模拟器 grounded 的自回归视频模型，在改变视觉外观的同时保留策略相关特征（如场景布局和动态对象的时间一致性）。同时提出 FDπ，一种多表示度量，用公开 E2E 策略的场景上下文特征上的 Fréchet 距离衡量 sim-to-real 差距。基于 DreamStream 构建 Navhard-CL，将非反应式真实基准 NAVSIM 转为含对抗驾驶行为和天气变化的交互测试环境。

Result: 在 FDπ 下，DreamStream 在 nuScenes 上比最强先前闭环模拟器提升 1.6×，在 NAVSIM 上提升 4.7×，并对策略感知可观测性造成最小扰动。Navhard-CL 暴露了先前闭环基准忽略的多种失败模式，如 scorer bias 和缺乏恢复行为。

Conclusion: DreamStream 能以策略导向的保真度支持更可靠的闭环驾驶策略评估；FDπ 比 FID 等感知指标更能反映策略相关特征保持程度；Navhard-CL 提供了更具交互性和对抗性的测试基准。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Faithfully evaluating end-to-end driving policies in simulation requires observations that are not merely photo-realistic, but preserve the scene features a policy relies on to make decisions. Existing platforms, however, exhibit a sim-to-real visual gap that corrupts policy perception, undermining their ability to assess a policy's closed-loop decision-making. To this end, we propose DreamStream, a generative, closed-loop simulator that achieves policy-oriented fidelity using a simulator-grounded autoregressive video model. Our video model is distilled from a large pretrained video model via traffic layout guidance, varying visual appearance while preserving policy-relevant features such as scenario layout and the temporal consistency of dynamic objects. We further observe that perceptual metrics like FID misrank how well these features are preserved. To tackle this, we introduce FD$\pi$, a new multi-representation metric that measures the sim-to-real gap as the Fréchet distance over scene-context features from public E2E policies. Under FD$\pi$, DreamStream improves over the strongest prior closed-loop simulator by $1.6\times$ on nuScenes and $4.7\times$ on NAVSIM, and induces the least perturbation to policy's perceptual observability. Based on DreamStream, we construct Navhard-CL benchmark, which turns non-reactive real-world benchmark NAVSIM into interactive testing environments with adversarial driving behaviors and weather variations. This benchmark exposes many failure modes of driving policies, such as scorer bias and lack of recovery behaviors, that prior closed-loop benchmarks overlook. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [TM-APR: Thermal Temporal-Memory Localization via Analytic Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26766)
*Yanshuo Bai,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作首次将解析类增量学习（ACIL）与域不变热视觉位置识别（Thermal VPR）结合，并基于ACIL与现代控制理论的代数等价，提出U-ACIL、GMM-ACIL和H∞-ACIL，实现O(1)闭式在线更新、低时延且无轨迹跳变的实时SLAM热VPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热VPR是自主导航的前提，但现有方法受严重环境依赖、沉重在线重训练开销和无法建模动态非线性偏移的影响，导致在线部署失败，因此需要鲁棒的域不变位置识别。

Method: 首次桥接ACIL与域不变VPR，发现其无梯度矩阵更新可形成优于传统微调的强基线；针对标准ACIL因线性结构假设而对极端非线性热波动脆弱的问题，利用ACIL与现代控制理论的新颖代数等价，将无迹传播（U-ACIL）、高斯混合分区（GMM-ACIL）和极小极大H∞优化（H∞-ACIL）直接嵌入更新循环，保证O(1)复杂度下的精确闭式矩阵更新并绕过反向传播。

Result: 所提框架保证在线更新时延Δt_learn严格低于传感器采集间隔Δt_acquire，从而消除实时SLAM管线中的轨迹跳变，并克服标准ACIL在极端非线性热波动下的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该工作为热VPR在线部署提供了高效、鲁棒且域不变的解决方案，并通过连接增量学习与控制理论，提升了实时SLAM的稳定性与可行性。

Abstract: Thermal Visual Place Recognition (Thermal VPR) maps camera observations to metric poses within a mapped environment, serving as a prerequisite for autonomous navigation. However, thermal VPR suffers from severe environmental dependence, heavy online retraining overheads, and an inability to model dynamic non-linear shifts, causing existing frameworks to fail during online deployment. To achieve robust domain-invariant place recognition, we bridge Analytic Class-Incremental Learning (ACIL) with domain-invariant VPR for the first time, revealing that its gradient-free matrix updates construct a surprisingly strong baseline that outperforms conventional fine-tuning. Nevertheless, standard ACIL exhibits a critical vulnerability to extreme non-linear thermal fluctuations due to its structural linear assumptions. To overcome this limitation, we exploit a novel algebraic equivalence between ACIL and modern control theory, proposing a framework which embeds Unscented propagation (U-ACIL), Gaussian Mixture partitioning (GMM-ACIL), and minimax $H_\infty$ optimization ($H_\infty$-ACIL) directly into the update loop. Our formulation guarantees exact closed-form matrix updates within $\mathcal{O}(1)$ computational complexity, bypassing backpropagation to ensure that the online update latency ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{learn}}$) remains strictly bounded below the sensor acquisition interval ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{acquire}}$), thereby eliminating trajectory jumps in real-time SLAM pipelines.

</details>


### [9] [Underwater Navigation in Unsteady Flows Using Measurement Histories from a Single Sensing Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26753)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 单点历史流观测器可从单个传感单元的有限历史测量中估计侧向流速，并在未重新训练的情况下为固定导航控制器提供接近直接空间感知的闭环导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下导航依赖空间流场测量，但分布式感知受机器人尺寸与传感器布局限制；因此需要减少同步流采样点，并利用单传感单元的历史信息实现有效导航。

Method: 在二维尾流仿真中，使用因果观测器从单传感单元有限历史测量中估计当前侧向速度，作为固定导航控制器输入；仅在 Re=100 圆柱尾流训练。该虚拟感知接口将同步流采样从三点减至机器人中心一点。研究对比直接空间感知与仅当前流观测器，并测试未见 Re、不同障碍物几何、组件替换及受控扰动。

Result: 在未重新训练的 Re=205 和 240 下，成功率分别为 84.4% 和 80.6%，比直接空间感知低 7.4 和 4.6 个百分点，比匹配的仅当前流观测器高超过 30 个百分点。提供过去目标与偏航信息时，过去流仍有增益。方形棱柱尾流中性能接近直接感知，但三角棱柱尾流中下降。组件替换表明侧向速度差为控制相关分量，受控扰动显示对误差持续性敏感。

Conclusion: 在假定观测模型下，单点流历史对闭环导航具有实用价值，可减少同时流采样需求；但其性能受障碍物几何泛化与误差持续性影响。

Abstract: Spatial flow measurements support underwater navigation, but distributed sensing is constrained by robot size and sensor layout. We use a causal observer to estimate current lateral velocities from a finite history of measurements collected by a single sensing unit, supplying the inputs of a fixed navigation controller. In two-dimensional wake simulations with access to body-frame ambient velocity, this virtual sensing interface reduces simultaneous flow sampling from three points to the robot center. Trained only in a circular-cylinder wake at Re = 100, the flow-history observer achieves 84.4% and 80.6% success at held-out Re = 205 and 240 without retraining. These rates are 7.4 and 4.6 percentage points below direct spatial sensing and more than 30 points above a matched current-only observer. Past flow remains beneficial when past goal and yaw information is available. Across obstacle geometries, performance remains close to direct sensing in square-prism wakes but declines in triangular-prism wakes. Component replacement identifies the lateral velocity difference as control-relevant, while controlled perturbations reveal sensitivity to error persistence. The results demonstrate the closed-loop utility of single-point flow histories under the assumed observation model.

</details>


### [10] [Imperfection for Precision: Upcycling Imperfect Data for High-Precision Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26672)
*Hao Wei,Yang Liu,Chao Tang,Shengbao Li,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Zhaofeng Cao,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ε4P 方法，通过“升级利用”低精度目标任务数据和高精度不匹配任务数据，在 flow-matching 轨迹的不同噪声阶段分工，从而降低高精度操作对昂贵任务特定高质量数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练高精度视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常需要任务特定、高质量数据（如遥操作），但这类数据采集缓慢且昂贵。

Method: ε4P 将两类通常被丢弃的数据源加以利用：目标任务的低精度数据和任务不匹配的高精度数据。方法不在共训练中简单混合，而是控制各数据源在 flow-matching 轨迹中的贡献位置：高噪声阶段使用低精度目标任务数据以保留高层任务上下文，低噪声阶段使用高精度但不匹配任务的数据以迁移底层动作精度。

Result: 在亚毫米级高精度任务和粗粒度任务的真实机器人实验中，该方法能有效利用额外不完美数据，使策略性能最多提升 31.7 个百分点；并能替代等量任务特定高质量数据，平均性能仅下降 4.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: ε4P 指向一种可扩展的高精度操作范式：异构、不完美的数据可被系统性再利用，以减少对昂贵任务特定高质量数据的依赖。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) models for high-precision manipulation typically requires task-specific, high-quality data (e.g., teleoperation), which is slow and expensive to collect. To reduce this burden without compromising manipulation precision, we propose $\varepsilon$4P (Imperfection for Precision), a simple yet effective method that "upcycles" two otherwise discarded data sources: (1) low-precision data from the target task and (2) high-precision data from mismatched tasks. Rather than naively mixing these imperfect data sources throughout co-training, $\varepsilon$4P controls where each source contributes along the flow-matching trajectory. Specifically, low-precision, target-task data is used at high noise to preserve high-level task context and high-precision, task-mismatched data is used at low noise to transfer low-level action precision. Through real-robot experiments on both sub-millimeter, high-precision tasks and coarse-grained tasks, we demonstrate that the proposed method (1) effectively leverages additional imperfect data to improve policy performance by up to 31.7 percentage points, and (2) can replace an equal amount of task-specific, high-quality data with an average performance drop of only 4.2 percentage points. Overall, $\varepsilon$4P points toward a scalable paradigm for high-precision manipulation, in which heterogeneous, imperfect data can be systematically repurposed to reduce reliance on costly task-specific, high-quality data. More details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [11] [NavSafe-$\infty$: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Driving Safety in Photorealistic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26618)
*Yuxin Bao,Hongwei Ruan,Luobin Wang,Seth Z. Zhao,Ziyang Leng,Zihan Zhang,Yu Zeng,Rowan McAllister,Henrik Christensen,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环安全基准 NavSafe-∞，发现端到端驾驶策略的开环性能提升无法可靠转化为闭环安全性，常见补救措施也存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开环评测无法揭示策略能否承受累积误差、从失败中恢复以及与周围交通参与者安全交互，因此需要更贴近真实安全需求的闭环评测。

Method: 构建 NavSafe-∞：包含 280 个逼真闭环场景、覆盖 28 类事件，并在结构化交通安全分类法下定义成功与失败标准，输出交通碰撞、弱势道路使用者碰撞、交通违规和交通事件等类别能力分数；评测 20 个端到端策略，并分析被动示范扰动与开环强化学习微调两种常见补救方法。

Result: 开环收益不能可靠迁移到闭环安全；被动示范扰动仅在闭环 rollout 接近其扰动训练状态时主要有效；开环强化学习微调出现奖励黑客，以牺牲安全裕度换取自车进展，闭环反馈将其放大为累积性安全关键错误。

Conclusion: 开环基准在指示闭环安全成功方面存在盲点；NavSafe-∞ 及可扩展工具箱将开源维护，以支持可定制事件构建与策略诊断研究。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) driving policies have progressed rapidly on open-loop (OL) benchmarks, yet OL evaluation cannot reveal whether a policy withstands compounding errors, recovers from failures, or interacts safely with surrounding actors. We introduce NavSafe-$\infty$, a photorealistic closed-loop (CL) benchmark of 280 scenarios spanning 28 event types, each with success and failure criteria defined within a structured traffic-safety taxonomy, which yields category-level capability scores for Traffic Crashes, Vulnerable Road User Crashes, Traffic Violations, and Traffic Incidents. Evaluating 20 E2E policies, we find that OL gains do not reliably transfer to CL safety. Analyzing two common remedies further shows that passive demonstration perturbation helps mainly when CL rollouts stay near its perturbed training states, and that OL reinforcement-learning fine-tuning exhibits reward hacking by trading safety margin for ego progress, which CL feedback amplifies into compounding safety-critical errors. Together, these results demonstrate the blind spot of OL benchmarks indicating CL safety success. The benchmark and an extensible toolbox for customizable event curation and policy diagnosis will be open-sourced and maintained to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [12] [Wheel-loader V-Cycle Automation with Deep Koopman MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26580)
*Armin Abdolmohammadi,Navid Mojahed,Dinesh Kumar,Bahram Ravani,Shima Nazari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层框架，结合长时域几何规划与数据驱动预测控制，实现轮式装载机自主V型循环作业，仿真验证了其准确性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式装载机在土方作业中反复进行前进-后退操作，适合自动化，但铰接式车辆的非线性动力学和复杂的车辆-地形交互限制了传统基于模型方法的有效性。

Method: 提出分层框架：使用降阶铰接运动学模型生成操作几何，通过共享中间状态联合优化前进和后退轨迹；基于Algoryx Dynamics高保真仿真数据，为前进和后退运动分别学习两个数据驱动的深度双线性Koopman模型；将Koopman表示嵌入计算高效的模型预测控制（MPC）中进行轨迹跟踪，控制器在50毫秒执行循环内实时运行。

Result: 高保真仿真结果表明，所提出的端到端框架能够准确且计算高效地执行轮式装载机V型循环作业。

Conclusion: 该研究为铰接式重型机械的自主操作提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: The repeated forward-reverse maneuvers performed by wheel loaders during earthmoving operations make them well suited for automation. However, the nonlinear dynamics of articulated vehicles and complex vehicle-terrain interactions limit the effectiveness of conventional model-based approaches. This paper presents a hierarchical framework that combines long-horizon geometric planning with data-driven predictive control for autonomous wheel-loader operation. A reduced-order articulated kinematic model is used to generate the maneuver geometry, where the forward and reverse trajectories are jointly optimized through a shared intermediate state. To capture the vehicle dynamics, two data-driven deep bilinear Koopman models are learned for the forward and reverse motions using data generated from high-fidelity simulations in Algoryx Dynamics. The learned Koopman representations are subsequently incorporated into a computationally efficient model predictive control (MPC) formulation for trajectory tracking. The resulting controller operates in real time within a 50-ms execution loop. High-fidelity simulation results demonstrate that the proposed end-to-end framework enables accurate and computationally efficient execution of wheel-loader V-cycle maneuvers, providing a promising approach toward autonomous operation of articulated heavy-duty machinery.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond End-Task Success: How to Audit Visual Experience Retrieval in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26567)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出现有经验复用系统仅报告所选经验成功率，无法区分选择规则优劣与经验库质量；提出穷举审计方法，在2个操作任务、3种复用机制、K=3/10/50经验库上执行每个经验于每个查询场景，并用5种视觉嵌入的最近邻选择规则进行审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通过复用过去经验而非重训练来适应新场景，但多数系统按视觉相似度选择经验，且仅报告所选经验成功率。该指标可能因反复使用一个广泛可迁移经验而虚高，或因偏好的经验本身弱而偏低；它描述的是经验库而非选择规则，会误导领域后续构建。

Method: 审计方法：对每个查询场景穷举执行库中每个经验，从而已知所有候选结果，可将分数归因于逐场景选择或经验库质量。覆盖2个操作任务、3种复用机制、K=3、10、50；被审计规则基于5种视觉嵌入（从原始像素到CLIP）的最近邻距离选择经验；并设置保持选择率但随机配对的shuffle对照。

Result: (1) 事后选定的单个固定经验可弥补随机选择与oracle之间30-58%的差距，逐场景选择仅竞争剩余0.07-0.15成功率。(2) K≥10时，视觉规则对某一经验的集中度比oracle高1.5-3倍，其分数随后跟随该经验质量。(3) 凡规则显著差于保持选择率但随机配对的shuffle处，所有学习图像策略均更差。(4) 视觉距离能较好预测给定经验-场景对是否成功（AUROC达0.96），但在K=50时，5种嵌入中有4种在单场景内对候选排序不优于随机（AUROC 0.45-0.52）。

Conclusion: 穷举执行通常不可行，因此审计可简化为任何研究都能给出的两个低成本报告：所选经验的分布，以及事后最佳单一经验的成功率。这能揭示选择规则是否真正有效，而非仅依赖所选经验的成功数字。

Abstract: Robots that store past experiences must select which one to reuse in a new scene. Most systems select by visual similarity, and most evaluations report only the success of the selected experience. That number does not show whether the selection was good: a rule can score well by repeatedly using one broadly transferable experience, or poorly because its preferred experience is weak. Since robots increasingly adapt by reuse rather than retraining, a score that describes the library rather than the rule misleads what the field builds next. We contribute an audit methodology: execute every stored experience in every query scene, over two manipulation tasks, three reuse mechanisms, and libraries of $K=3$, $10$, and $50$. Because every alternative's outcome is known, a score can be traced to per-scene selection or to library quality. The audited rules select by nearest-neighbor distance in five visual embeddings, from raw pixels to CLIP. (1) One fixed experience, chosen with hindsight, captures 30-58% of the gap between random selection and an oracle; per-scene selection competes for the remaining 0.07-0.15 in success rate. (2) At $K\ge10$, visual rules concentrate on one experience 1.5-3 times more than the oracle does, and their scores then follow that experience's quality. (3) Wherever a rule differs significantly from a shuffle that keeps its selection rates but pairs them with scenes at random, the rule is worse, for every learned image policy. (4) Visual distance predicts well whether a given pair will succeed (AUROC up to 0.96), yet ranks the candidates within one scene no better than chance for four of five embeddings at $K=50$ (AUROC 0.45-0.52). Exhaustive execution is usually infeasible, so the audit reduces to two cheap reports any study can give: the distribution of selected experiences, and the success of the best single experience in hindsight.

</details>


### [14] [Learning Air-Ground Motion Control with Temporal Mode Switching and Cross-Terrain Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26564)
*Ruitian Pang,Mingrui Li,Xuanting Liu,Tiancheng Lai,Juncheng Chen,Xiangyu Li,Ruibin Zhang,Qishao Wang,Jin Yu,Haiyin Piao,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向被动轮式陆空双模态车辆的学习型空地运动控制框架，实现受限感知下的自主模态切换与多地形鲁棒地面轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮式TABV兼具空中机动与地面高效移动优势，但在有限机载感知下可靠空地切换、以及跨多样地形的鲁棒地面轨迹跟踪仍是实际应用难题。

Method: 框架包含：1）学习型模式选择器，利用历史单点ToF测量、机器人状态与未来参考信息决定当前运动模式；2）基于强化学习的轨迹跟踪控制策略，将本体感知与未来参考结合以预判轨迹变化；地面运动采用多地形训练与动力学随机化增强鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明：受限感知下空地切换可靠，多地形地面跟踪准确；学习型选择器在困难切换中优于规则选择器；地面控制器在所有测试条件下位置RMSE低于PID，并在NMPC失败时仍保持可接受跟踪；集成系统完成101m空地轨迹、多次自主模态切换，位置RMSE为0.08m。

Conclusion: 该学习型控制框架可实现可靠自主空地模态切换与跨地形鲁棒地面跟踪，性能优于规则/PID/NMPC基线，展示了实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Passive-wheeled terrestrial-aerial bimodal vehicles (TABVs) combine aerial mobility with energy-efficient ground locomotion. However, reliable air-ground mode switching under limited onboard perception and robust ground trajectory tracking across diverse terrains remain challenging when targeting real-world applications. In this work, we propose a learning-based air-ground motion control framework for passive-wheeled TABVs: 1) a learned mode selector for autonomous air-ground motion mode switching. The selector uses historical single-point time-of-flight (ToF) measurements and robot states together with future reference information to determine the active locomotion mode. 2) a reinforcement learning control policy for trajectory tracking. The policy combines proprioceptive observations with future reference information to anticipate trajectory changes. For ground locomotion, multi-terrain training and dynamics randomization enable robust tracking across different terrains. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate reliable air-ground switching under limited perception and accurate ground tracking across diverse terrain conditions. The learned selector outperforms a rule-based mode selector in challenging transitions, while the ground controller achieves lower position RMSE than PID across all tested conditions and maintains decent tracking where NMPC fails. With these capabilities integrated, the system tracks a 101m air-ground trajectory through multiple autonomous mode transitions with a position RMSE of 0.08m.

</details>


### [15] [MATE: Multi-Agent Virtual Teleoperation Platform for Humanoid Collaboration Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26520)
*Yichuan Yu,Youzhuo Wang,Yiming Ren,Di Feng,Yexuan Yang,Bingxi Yang,Shengxiao Gong,Yujing Sun,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MATE多智能体虚拟遥操作平台，让多地操作员在共享物理仿真环境中同时控制全身人形机器人，采集多机协作数据；并引入EAIS采样策略提升交互示范学习效率，实现从虚拟演示到物理人形机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要多样化的具身经验来学习复杂移动操作与协作技能，但现有数据管线主要面向单智能体；物理多机器人协作受硬件成本高、专用空间需求大、重复重置频繁等问题限制，难以规模化采集数据。

Method: 构建MATE：面向人形机器人协作数据采集的多智能体虚拟遥操作平台，支持多个地理分布操作员同时控制共享物理仿真环境中的全身人形机器人，无需多台物理机器人及同地操作，同时保留人形机器人、物体与环境间的物理耦合交互。基于该平台构建包含24.1小时协调行为、2500个联合回合、5类长时程任务（物体交接、接力递送、环境交互、协同运输等）的多机协作数据集。提出EAIS执行对齐交互采样策略，在执行对齐前缀内计算采样信号，优先采样推进任务进度和交互关键的行为。使用代表性模仿学习与视觉-语言-动作策略在多种协作任务上评估。

Result: 实验表明MATE能够高效采集数据，有效学习协作策略，并可将在虚拟演示中训练的策略零样本迁移到物理人形机器人，无需真实世界微调。

Conclusion: MATE通过多智能体虚拟遥操作解决了物理多机协作数据采集的规模化难题，EAIS提升了交互丰富示范的学习效果，验证了虚拟协作数据向真实人形机器人策略迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require diverse embodied experiences to acquire complex loco-manipulation and collaborative skills. However, existing humanoid data pipelines primarily focus on individual agents, while physical multi-robot collaboration remains difficult to scale due to costly hardware, dedicated spaces, and repeated resets. In this work, we introduce MATE, a Multi-Agent virtual TEleoperation platform for humanoid collaboration data collection that enables multiple geographically distributed operators to simultaneously control whole-body humanoids in a shared physics-based environment. MATE removes the need for multiple physical robots and co-located operation while preserving physically coupled interactions among humanoids, objects, and environments. Using MATE, we construct a multi-humanoid collaboration dataset comprising 24.1 hours of coordinated behavior across 2,500 joint episodes and five long-horizon tasks, including object handover, relay delivery, environment interaction, and cooperative transport. To improve learning from these interaction-rich demonstrations, we introduce EAIS, an Execution-Aligned Interaction Sampling strategy that computes sampling signals within an execution-aligned prefix and prioritizes task-progressing and interaction-critical behaviors. We evaluate MATE with representative imitation learning and vision-language-action policies across diverse collaboration tasks. Experiments demonstrate efficient data collection, effective policy learning, and zero-shot transfer from virtual demonstrations to a physical humanoid without real-world fine-tuning. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [16] [Generalizing Manipulation Skills with a Local Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26499)
*Raman Talwar,Elias Nijs,Andreas Verleysen,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本地开放权重视觉语言模型通过编码智能体框架驱动UR3e机械臂，在无需新增人类编程或训练的情况下对未见任务变体实现一次性泛化，45次试验中30次成功，并观察到复做任务耗时减半的自我改进现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言驱动机器人通常依赖固定动作接口或训练策略，泛化到新任务需额外工程或数据采集，耗时且昂贵。本文探究本地开放权重VLM能否直接控制机器人，并零样本/一次性泛化到任务新变体。

Method: 使用本地开放权重VLM Qwen3.8-27B，通过编码智能体harness驱动UR3e机械臂。模型自行编写并运行代码，底层服务提供运动学、安全限制和经典计算机视觉。基于儿童玩具设计九个任务，覆盖颜色、尺寸、形状及任务变化，每任务5次试验；并测试成功完成后复做任务是否加速。

Result: 45次试验中30次成功泛化；单次耗时3.4至67.5分钟，取决于任务复杂度。成功复做任务时耗时减少50%，表明随时间自我改进。同时揭示本地编码智能体的局限性。

Conclusion: 解决这些局限并结合对自我改进的进一步研究，指向本地编码智能体在真实世界部署的直接路径。

Abstract: Today, progress in open-weight language models enables systems capable of writing, executing and debugging code while still running on a single workstation. Most language-driven robots give the model a fixed action interface or a trained policy. Generalizing to a new task therefore means more engineering effort or more data collection, both time-consuming. We investigate whether a local open-weight vision-language model can control a robot and one-shot generalize to new variations of a task without new human programming or training. We let a local open-weight VLM, Qwen3.8-27B, drive a UR3e robotic arm from a coding-agent harness. It writes and runs its own code above a service that implements kinematics, safety limits and classic computer vision techniques. We investigate if this system is capable of generalizing to unseen tasks. Specifically, we test it on nine tasks built from children's toys designed to probe generalization capability across various object characteristics: color, size, shape, and task variation of those. With five trials for each task, we observe generalization in 30 out of 45 trials with durations ranging from 3.4 to 67.5 minutes depending on task complexity. We further test if there is a speedup when an agent is asked to redo the task after successful completion. This resulted in a 50% reduction in duration, indicating that there is self-improvement over time. Finally, we expose the limitations of a local coding agent. We believe that solving those limitations combined with further investigation of self-improvement over time points at a direct path toward real-world deployment of a local coding agent.

</details>


### [17] [Benchmarking Robots for Everyday Environments: From Lab Experiments to Real-World Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26490)
*Raphael Memmesheimer,Martina Overbeck,Dominik Beyer,Björn Kral,Sabine Bellmann,Sven Schneider,Jan Zimmermann,Anna-Maria Meer,Medina Klicic,Simone Roth,Carolin Straßmann,Alexander Arntz,Marlene Wessels,Johannes Kraus,Paul Schweidler,Tristan Schnell,Christoph Zimmermann,Benedikt Pulver,Wilhelm Stork,Martin Gersch,Sven Behnke,Arne Rönnau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出面向公共环境部署机器人的跨学科基准测试框架，通过2023—2025年七次基准活动，评估户外公园清洁、人行地下通道清洁和图书馆互动辅助三类机器人用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验室指标不足以衡量机器人在非结构化、以人为中心的真实公共环境中的部署表现，需弥合实验室原型与实际运行系统之间的基准测试差距。

Method: 组建涵盖机器人学、人机交互、安全与经济学领域的专家组，系统制定并迭代评估概念；在三年内开展七次基准活动，包括一次共识研讨会和六次现场评估（每个用例两次），利用真实的室内外测试环境，评估三类不同机器人在公共日常场景中的技术性能及更广泛部署影响。

Result: 研究识别出成功部署的关键因素，包括任务完成度、交互质量、安全性和经济可行性，并分析了从实验室原型向可运行系统转化的核心要求。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究人员和从业者提供了可操作洞见，有助于弥合机器人创新与真实世界适用性之间的差距，并为公共环境机器人部署评估提供跨学科基准测试参考。

Abstract: This study introduces an interdisciplinary framework for benchmarking robots deployed in public environments, addressing the gap between traditional laboratory metrics and real-world benchmarking requirements. We evaluate three distinct robots across diverse use cases - outdoor park cleaning, pedestrian underpass cleaning, and interactive library assistance - each representing unique challenges in public daily life. Over a three-year benchmarking process (2023-2025) comprising seven benchmarking events, a consensus workshop and six on-site evaluations (two per use case), we utilized realistic indoor and outdoor test environments to assess not only technical performance but also the broader implications of deploying robots in unstructured, human-centric settings. An expert panel, spanning robotics, human-robot interaction, safety, and economics, systematically developed and refined an evaluation concept to analyze the transition from laboratory prototypes to operational systems. Our findings highlight critical factors for successful deployment, including task fulfillment, interaction quality, safety, and economic feasibility. This work provides actionable insights for researchers and practitioners aiming to bridge the gap between robotic innovation and real-world applicability.

</details>


### [18] [RouteRLT: Learning When and Which RL Specialist Should Control a Vision-Language-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26467)
*Chongyu Zhu,Jaden Hinds,Hyegang Kim,Juan Sebastian Rojas,Ramy Elmallah,Chi-Guhn Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RouteRLT 路由框架，使通用 VLA 在精度关键阶段自动切换至相应 RL 专家策略，同时保持通用操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通用操作能力强，但在连接器插入、线缆整理等接触密集工业任务的精度关键阶段表现不佳。常见做法是用 RL 微调预训练 VLA，但如何保留通用行为、判断何时需要 RL 精调以及选择哪个专家策略仍是待解问题。

Method: RouteRLT 学习何时以及哪个 RL specialist 接管控制。每个 RL specialist 针对单个精度关键阶段训练。框架包含 phase selector 识别当前活动控制器，stabilizer 抑制瞬态切换，action-boundary manager 处理分块策略输出间的过渡。

Result: 在 LIBERO 多物体 pick-and-place 仿真任务和真实线缆拾取与端口插入任务（含多个精度关键阶段）上评估。仿真中学习路由优于基础 VLA，并达到使用特权阶段边界路由的相当性能，而部署时无需访问这些边界。真实机器人验证了在操作员对齐交接协议下自动路由到拾取和插入专家。

Conclusion: 学习路由能在精确适配最有价值的阶段应用 RL 专家控制，同时保留通用 VLA 行为，并支持从失败执行尝试中恢复。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad manipulation competence, but often struggle during the precision-critical stages that dominate contact-rich industrial tasks such as connector insertion and cable management. A common remedy is to refine a pretrained VLA with reinforcement learning (RL), enabling task-specific improvement beyond behavior cloning. However, how to preserve its generalist behavior while deciding when RL refinement is needed and which specialized policy should act remains an open question. In this work, we present RouteRLT, a routing framework that learns when and which RL specialist, an RL policy trained for a single precision-critical phase, should take control from a generalist VLA. A phase selector identifies the active controller, a stabilizer suppresses transient switches, and an action-boundary manager handles transitions between chunked policy outputs. We evaluate RouteRLT on multi-object pick-and-place tasks in LIBERO, as well as on a real-world cable pickup and port-insertion task with multiple precision-critical stages. In simulation, the learned routing improves over the base VLA and matches routing with privileged phase boundaries, without accessing those boundaries at deployment. The real-robot evaluation validates automatic routing to both the pickup and insertion specialists under an operator-aligned handoff protocol. Altogether, these results show that learned routing applies RL specialist control where precise adaptation is most valuable while preserving generalist VLA behavior, including recovery from failed execution attempts.

</details>


### [19] [Dr-LiSA: Direct Radar-Lidar Scan Alignment for $SE(3)$ Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26423)
*Alex Zhang,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Dr-LiSA，首个将 2D 旋转雷达强度测量在 SE(3) 中直接定位到 3D 激光雷达地图的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达对恶劣天气和降水鲁棒，激光雷达可提供高保真 3D 地图，二者互补。但现有雷达-激光雷达定位多局限于平面 SE(2)，精度通常低于激光-激光甚至雷达-雷达系统，核心挑战是雷达与激光雷达在场景结构观测和表示上存在显著模态鸿沟。

Method: Dr-LiSA 使用学习到的前向模型，在候选位姿处从激光雷达子图预测雷达测量，从而在 SE(3) 中对预测雷达扫描与观测雷达扫描进行直接光度对齐，实现雷达-激光雷达直接定位。

Result: 在超过 90 km 的道路数据上，Dr-LiSA 在 SE(2) 下优于先前雷达-激光雷达方法，并达到与当前最优雷达-雷达定位相当的平面精度。

Conclusion: Dr-LiSA 是首个面向雷达-激光雷达的 SE(3) 直接定位方法，通过可学习前向模型弥合模态差异，推动了雷达-激光雷达定位向三维和更高精度发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-LiSA, a first-of-its-kind direct method for localizing 2D spinning radar intensity measurements in $SE(3)$ against 3D lidar maps. Radar-lidar localization combines the complementary strengths of the two sensing modalities: radar is robust to adverse weather and precipitation, while lidar provides high-fidelity 3D maps in favourable conditions. However, existing radar-lidar localization methods are restricted to planar $SE(2)$ localization and have generally fallen short of the accuracy achieved by lidar-lidar and even radar-radar systems. A key challenge is the substantial sensing-modality gap between radar and lidar, which observe and represent scene structure in fundamentally different ways. Dr-LiSA bridges this gap using a learned forward model that predicts radar measurements from a lidar submap at a candidate pose, enabling direct photometric alignment of predicted and observed radar scans in $SE(3)$. Dr-LiSA outperforms prior radar-lidar approaches in $SE(2)$ while achieving planar accuracy competitive with state-of-the-art radar-radar localization across more than 90 km of on-road data.

</details>


### [20] [Sample, Simulate, Select: Physics-in-the-Loop Text-to-Motion for Humanoids Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26420)
*Raphael Memmesheimer,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出无需训练的 Sample-simulate-select（S³）框架：从冻结的文本到动作模型为每个提示采样 N 个动作，经方向匹配逆运动学重定向到 Unitree G1，再用预训练的 SONIC 全身跟踪策略在完整刚体动力学中仿真滚动，选择策略执行最好的候选。由于验证器就是确定性仿真器，S³ 按构造达到 any-of-N 上限，并在 HumanML3D 上显著提升可执行率与硬件门控通过率，真实 G1 上所有门控片段均完成站立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到动作模型能生成合理人体动作，却不建模机器人动力学；全身跟踪控制器能可靠执行可行参考，却无法重新规划不可行参考。近期语言到人形系统通过训练弥合该差距，本文则测量：完全不训练、仅把部署控制器放进闭环，能缩小多少差距。

Method: S³ 对每个提示从冻结的文本到动作模型采样 N 个动作，用方向匹配逆运动学将每个动作重定向到 Unitree G1，再以预训练 SONIC 跟踪策略在完整刚体动力学下展开所有候选，并保留策略执行最佳的候选。因为验证器是确定性仿真器本身，S³ 按构造达到 any-of-N 上限；实验进一步用标准文本-动作评估器衡量执行动作的语义保真度，以真实动捕作为对照归因机器人投影带来的损失，并针对重定向器与 GMR 做消融，最后在真实 G1 上执行全部门控选中的片段。

Result: 在 200 个分层 HumanML3D 测试提示、N=8 时，直立执行率从 83.5% 提升到 89.5%，硬件门控通过数从 33 提升到 85；在完整测试集（4,184 个提示）上从 80.5% 提升到 89.5%。一个预测跌倒良好的运动学验证器（AUROC 0.90）仅恢复约四分之一增益，说明对同一提示的候选排序比总体分类更难。选择无法修复的一类失败是降低骨盆的提示，而基于重定向机器人数据训练的生成器可以执行该类动作。重定向器与 GMR 消融显示互补失败，两者合并后 any-of-8 上限升至 95.0%。在真实 G1 上执行全部 177 个门控选中片段，每个都保持站立完成，硬件跟踪误差与仿真匹配（r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 无需训练的部署闭环选择能显著缩小文本到动作生成与机器人执行之间的差距，并逼近 any-of-N 上限；但选择受候选集与生成器分布限制，尤其对降低骨盆类提示无效。确定性仿真验证使选择可靠，真实硬件结果验证了可迁移性；进一步收益需针对失败类别改进生成或重定向，而非仅靠选择。

Abstract: Text-to-motion models generate plausible human motion but do not model a robot's dynamics; whole-body tracking controllers execute robot references reliably but cannot replan an infeasible one. Recent language-to-humanoid systems bridge this gap by training. We measure how much of the gap closes with no training at all, by putting the deployment controller itself in the loop. Sample-simulate-select (S$^3$) draws $N$ motions per prompt from a frozen text-to-motion model, retargets each to a Unitree G1 by direction-matching inverse kinematics, rolls all of them out under full rigid-body dynamics with the pretrained SONIC tracking policy, and keeps the candidate the policy executed best. Because the verifier is the deterministic simulator itself, S$^3$ attains the any-of-$N$ ceiling by construction; what we measure is where that ceiling lies and what falls short of it. On 200 stratified HumanML3D test prompts with $N=8$, upright execution rises from 83.5% to 89.5% and hardware-gate passes from 33 to 85; on the complete test split (4,184 prompts) it rises from 80.5% to 89.5%. A kinematic verifier that predicts falls well (AUROC 0.90) recovers only a quarter of this gain: ranking a prompt's own candidates is harder than classifying the population. What selection cannot fix is one class, prompts that lower the pelvis, which a generator trained on retargeted robot data does execute. We further score the semantic fidelity of the executed motion with the standard text-motion evaluator, with a real-mocap control that attributes the loss to the robot projection, ablate the retargeter against GMR (complementary failures: the any-of-8 ceiling rises to 95.0% over both), and execute all 177 gate-selected clips on the real G1: every one completes standing, with hardware tracking error matching simulation ($r=0.94$).

</details>


### [21] [SparseNav: Instruction-conditioned Sparse Semantic Perception for Training-Free Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26408)
*Quanhua Chen,Juhan Kang,Runfeng Lin,ZiFei Zhang,Enquang Feng,Chunran Zheng,Xiwang Dong,Jiarong Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SparseNav 是一种免训练、遵循“少即是多”原则的语义导航框架，通过轻量几何 BEV 地图与稀疏地标记忆，按当前子指令按需获取语义，实现高效的视觉语言导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于地图的视觉语言导航（VLN）依赖持久空间表示连接语言理解与几何规划，但获取超出当前指令需求的语义会带来额外感知开销与无关标注；持续累积无关物体会浪费计算，并使 VLM 规划器消费的视觉-空间表示变得杂乱。

Method: SparseNav 持久维护轻量几何鸟瞰图（BEV）地图和稀疏地标记忆，仅在需要时获取新语义。指令管理器跟踪导航进度并识别当前活跃的地标查询；指令条件感知机制在查询地标可见且其度量位置能指导下一步决策时调用开放词汇分割。所得地标记忆支持 VLM 在混合前沿与局部方向性航点候选之间进行选择，且无需额外训练。

Result: 在 Val-Unseen 划分上，SparseNav 在 R2R-CE 达到 42.8% 成功率，在 RxR-CE 达到 40.7% 成功率；消融实验考察了语义感知策略及各框架组件的贡献。作者还将 SparseNav 部署于搭载 Intel RealSense D455 RGB-D 相机（用于几何建图与地标定位）和 Livox MID-360 LiDAR（用于定位）的 Unitree Go2 四足机器人，无需预建地图，并在多个室内环境中通过指令条件航点导航验证有效性。

Conclusion: SparseNav 通过按需语义感知与稀疏地标记忆有效减少感知成本、无关标注与地图杂乱，在免训练条件下取得较好的 VLN 性能，并具备真实机器人部署能力。

Abstract: Map-based vision-language navigation (VLN) relies on persistent spatial representations to connect language understanding with geometric planning. However, acquiring semantics beyond the needs of the current instruction can introduce unnecessary perception cost and irrelevant annotations. Continuously accumulating unrelated objects may not only waste computation, but also clutter the visual-spatial representation consumed by the vision-language model (VLM) planner. To address this problem, we present SparseNav, a training-free framework that follows a less-is-more principle for semantic navigation. SparseNav persistently maintains a lightweight geometric bird's-eye-view (BEV) map and sparse landmark memory, acquiring new semantics on demand using the active sub-instruction to decide what is worth grounding. An instruction manager first tracks navigation progress and identifies the active landmark query. An instruction-conditioned perception mechanism then invokes open-vocabulary segmentation when the queried landmark is visible and its metric location can inform the next decision. The resulting landmark memory supports VLM selection among hybrid frontier and local directional waypoint candidates. Without any additional training, SparseNav achieves success rates of 42.8% on R2R-CE and 40.7% on RxR-CE, both on the Val-Unseen splits. Controlled ablations examine semantic perception strategies and the contributions of individual framework components. Furthermore, we successfully deployed SparseNav on a Unitree Go2 quadruped equipped with an Intel RealSense D455 RGB-D camera for geometric mapping and landmark grounding and a Livox MID-360 LiDAR for localization, without a prebuilt map. We validated its effectiveness across multiple indoor environments using instruction-conditioned waypoint navigation.

</details>


### [22] [MAVP: Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26378)
*Jinhe Tang,Ruixiao Dai,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAVP通过地图锚定的基座位姿预测与定位反馈跟踪，提升移动操作策略的执行可靠性与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需要基座与机械臂协同运动并保持准确空间定位；但基于示范训练的策略往往难以可靠实现预期基座运动，造成空间错位并导致后续操作失败。

Method: MAVP从遥操作示范中重建静态地图，并将示范基座轨迹统一表达在共享地图坐标系中，以提供一致的跨示范空间监督。执行时，策略输入RGB观测、关节状态和当前地图系基座位姿，联合预测目标基座位姿、手臂动作和夹爪动作；底层控制器利用前馈运动与位姿误差反馈跟踪预测的基座目标，从而修正执行偏差。训练中还引入位姿噪声增强，以提高策略对位姿输入误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在六个真实世界操作任务和三种策略家族上，MAVP在所有任务中的成功率均高于无锚定速度控制基线。

Conclusion: 显式预测地图系基座目标并结合定位反馈闭环跟踪，可有效缓解移动操作中的空间错位问题，提高策略执行可靠性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Successful mobile manipulation requires coordinated base and arm motion while maintaining accurate spatial positioning. However, demonstration-trained policies can struggle to realise the intended base motion reliably, leading to spatial misalignment and subsequent manipulation failures. We present MAVP (Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies), a framework that improves execution reliability by predicting explicit base-pose targets and tracking them using localisation feedback. MAVP reconstructs a static map from teleoperated demonstrations and expresses demonstrated base trajectories in a shared map frame, providing consistent spatial supervision across demonstrations. At execution time, the policy receives RGB observations, joint states, and the robot's current map-frame base pose, and jointly predicts target base poses, arm actions, and gripper actions. A low-level controller tracks the predicted base targets using feedforward motion and pose error feedback, enabling correction of execution deviations. We additionally use pose-noise augmentation during training to improve robustness to errors in the policy's pose input. Across six real-world manipulation tasks and three policy families, MAVP achieves higher task success rates than unanchored velocity control in all tasks. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [Hierarchical Floorplan-Guided Vision-Language Exploration for Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26360)
*Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HFLEX-EQA分层具身问答框架，融合在线场景图构建、VLM规划、语义前沿探索与户型图先验，在OpenEQA、ExploreEQA及四足机器人真实环境中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EQA方法虽结合VLM与语义地图或场景图，但探索通常仅由局部观测驱动，环境结构先验未被充分利用。

Method: HFLEX-EQA从RGB-D观测增量构建分层场景图与开放词汇占据图，使VLM联合推理场景图、任务相关视觉观测、探索历史和估计的拓扑户型图；并引入房间发现策略，利用户型图与开放词汇前沿语义，引导探索朝向语义相关但尚未观测的房间类型。

Result: 在OpenEQA和ExploreEQA基准上评估，并在真实室内环境的四足机器人上部署；结果表明结合VLM分层规划与结构户型图先验对EQA任务有益。

Conclusion: 将VLM驱动的分层规划与户型图结构先验结合，可提升EQA的探索与问答表现，并具备真实机器人部署可行性。

Abstract: Embodied Question Answering (EQA) requires an agent to explore a previously unseen environment, gather relevant information, and answer questions about the scene. Recent approaches leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with semantic maps or scene graphs to guide exploration. However, exploration is typically driven only by local observations, while structural priors about the environment remain largely unused. We propose HFLEX-EQA, a hierarchical EQA framework that combines online scene graph construction, VLM- based planning, semantic frontier exploration, and floorplan priors. The system incrementally builds a hierarchical scene graph and an open-vocabulary occupancy map from RGB-D observations, enabling a VLM to jointly reason over the scene graph, task-relevant visual observations, exploration history, and an estimated topological floorplan. Furthermore, we introduce a room-discovery strategy that leverages the floorplan and open-vocabulary frontier semantics to guide exploration toward semantically relevant yet currently unobserved room types. We evaluate HFLEX-EQA on the OpenEQA and ExploreEQA benchmarks and demonstrate deployment on a quadruped robot in real indoor environments. Our results demonstrate the benefit of combining VLM-based hierarchical planning with structural floorplan priors for the EQA task.

</details>


### [24] [ArborSplat: Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Orchards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26315)
*Alessandro Masini,Matteo Frosi,Mirko Usuelli,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArborSplat 是面向果园的在线语义 3DGS SLAM，利用 LiDAR 里程计、类别高度带约束和在线多视角语义融合，在苹果/梨园多季节数据上实现低于 0.5 m 的 ATE，并在 mIoU 与速度上优于 SGS-SLAM 和 GS3LAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园机器人需要保留树干、棚架、果实等细小但语义重要结构的地图；现有 3DGS SLAM 虽具有高光度保真度，但优化仍由外观驱动，薄结构像素可能从背景表面获得深度，导致图像语义迁移到 3D 点不可靠。

Method: 提出 ArborSplat，一种在线语义 3DGS SLAM 系统：使用 LiDAR 里程计进行跟踪，并直接在 Gaussian 地图上优化语义；以每个关键帧的 stereo 点云拟合地平面，并施加类别特定高度带约束；在线融合多视角证据为语义点云，同时剔除与局部地面或单目深度不一致的标签；类别约束细化将 Gaussian 容量留给欠代表结构，并在降低预算时提升树类训练视角精度。

Result: 在休眠、开花和采收期的苹果与梨园评估。完整路线下，12 次遍历的 ATE 均低于 0.5 m。在共享 301 帧片段上，训练视角 mIoU 比 SGS-SLAM 和 GS3LAM 高 0.23 至 0.50，留出视角 mIoU 高 0.15 至 0.36，运行速度快 1.7 至 7.5 倍；而 SemGauss-SLAM 在全部六组上均因 GPU 内存不足失败。

Conclusion: ArborSplat 能为果园机器人提供跟踪鲁棒、语义准确且高效的在线语义 3DGS SLAM 建图，尤其改善薄结构语义建图，并在精度和效率上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Orchard robots need maps that preserve small but semantically important structures such as trunks, trellises, and fruit. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM achieves high photometric fidelity. However, its optimization remains appearance-driven, and transferring image semantics to 3D points is unreliable for thin structures, whose pixels may receive depth from background surfaces. We present ArborSplat, an online semantic 3DGS SLAM system that tracks with LiDAR odometry and optimizes semantics directly on the Gaussian map, constrained by class-specific height bands above a ground plane fitted to each keyframe's stereo point cloud, and fuses multi-view evidence into a semantic point cloud online while rejecting labels inconsistent with the local ground surface or with monocular depth. Class-constrained refinement reserves Gaussian capacity for underrepresented structures and, under reduced budgets, increases training-view accuracy on tree classes. We evaluate the approach on apple and pear orchards during dormancy, flowering, and harvesting. On full routes, it keeps ATE below 0.5 m on all 12 traversals. On shared 301-frame segments, it exceeds SGS-SLAM and GS3LAM by 0.23 to 0.50 training-view and 0.15 to 0.36 held-out mIoU while running 1.7 to 7.5 times faster, whereas SemGauss-SLAM runs out of GPU memory on all six.

</details>


### [25] [TriWorldBench: A Tri-View Consistency Perspective on Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26314)
*Xuanyi Liu,Haofeng Wang,Ruiqi Li,Danni Yu,Rui Wan,Ruixu Zhang,Siyu Tao,Xue Yang,Shaofeng Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TRIWORLDBENCH：一个通过同步头戴、左腕和右腕视频评估具身世界模型的基准，包含 500 个 episode、50 个双手操作任务、19 项指标及 TWB-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人具备头戴与腕部相机时，头视角提供整体任务信息，腕视角揭示局部抓取-物体交互；但独立评估各视角无法判断它们是否描述同一动作与物体状态，因此需要跨视角一致的评估方法。

Method: 构建同步三视角视频基准，覆盖 50 个双手操作任务、500 个 episode；使用 19 项指标评估三视角一致性、任务对齐、物理与 3D 连贯性、运动质量、时间一致性和视觉质量；结合跨视角检查与针对各相机的度量，并用 TWB-Score 汇总整体性能，同时保留各视角结果以定位预测失败。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数值或模型排名；主要结果是发布 TRIWORLDBENCH 基准、指标定义、代码和数据，并说明其可评估单个视频合理时是否仍能形成对目标任务的一致预测。

Conclusion: 该工作将世界模型评估从单视角视觉质量扩展到三视角一致性与任务对齐，有助于更可靠地评估具身世界模型对机器人学习与规划的支持能力。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the outcomes of robot actions to support learning and planning. For robots equipped with head and wrist cameras, this requires complementary views: the head view captures the overall task, while wrist views reveal local gripper-object interactions. However, evaluating these views independently cannot determine whether they describe the same action and object state. We introduce TRIWORLDBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating embodied world models through synchronized head, left-wrist, and right-wrist videos. It contains 500 episodes across 50 bimanual manipulation tasks and uses 19 metrics to assess tri-view consistency, task alignment, physical and 3D coherence, motion quality, temporal consistency, and visual quality. By combining cross-view checks with measurements tailored to each camera, the benchmark evaluates whether plausible individual videos also form a consistent prediction of the intended task. We summarize overall performance with TWB-Score and retain per-view results to identify where predictions fail. This extends world-model evaluation beyond single-view visual quality. Code, data, and metric definitions are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [SafeLoop: Risk-Aware Rollback for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26313)
*Zeyu Lou,Tianran Zhang,Xinquan Yue,Ya Jing,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeLoop 是一种非侵入式外部包装器，为 VLA 模型增加危险预测与基于回滚的恢复能力，无需修改原模型参数，从而提升长时程操作的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在通用操作上很有前景，但长时程执行仍较脆弱；微小的状态估计或控制误差可能导致碰撞、掉物等不可逆失败，因此需要能主动预见危险的安全机制。

Method: SafeLoop 从视觉和本体感知训练风险预测器，输出身体碰撞与物体失败的“发生概率”和“剩余时间到危险”；轻量控制器据此选择继续执行（noop）、保存安全检查点（record）或关节空间回退（rollback）。rollback 将机器人退回最近安全路点并重新查询基础策略，可能得到替代的后续动作。

Result: 在 24 个 LIBERO 任务（每个 16 个随机种子）和 3 个真实机器人任务（每个 25 次 rollout）上，SafeLoop 取得更优的安全-成功权衡，危险情况约减少 70%，同时保持任务成功率和基础策略控制率。

Conclusion: SafeLoop 可作为通用 VLA 的非侵入式安全增强模块，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下显著降低长时程操作风险；项目代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models are promising for general-purpose manipulation, but long-horizon execution remains fragile. Small state-estimation or control errors can lead to irreversible failures (e.g., collisions and object drops). Avoiding these risks requires a proactive safety mechanism capable of anticipating hazards. In this paper, we introduce SafeLoop, a non-invasive external wrapper that adds hazard prediction and rollback-based recovery to a VLA model without changing its parameters. SafeLoop trains a risk predictor from vision and proprioception to output four values: the probability and time-to-hazard for body collisions and for object failures. A lightweight controller then chooses one of three actions based on the predicted risk: continue execution (noop), save a safety checkpoint (record), or retreat in joint space (rollback). Rollback moves the robot back to a recent safe waypoint and queries the base policy again, which may yield an alternative continuation. Across 24 LIBERO tasks (16 random seeds each) and three real-robot tasks (25 rollouts each), SafeLoop achieves a stronger overall safety-success trade-off than alternative methods, reducing hazard cases by roughly 70% while preserving task success and the base-policy control rate. Project code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [27] [Shaft-Configuration-Adaptive Catheter Tip Position Estimation via Motor-History Conditioned Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26304)
*Peihan Zhang,Michael C. Yip,Ankur Kapoor,Young-Ho Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种电机历史条件化的GRU残差估计器，在无直接轴配置传感下实现三维导管尖端定位；用初始多向扫掠校准几何模型并生成轴配置上下文向量，后续仅凭电机测量预测任务空间残差；在4根一次性心内超声导管、16种弯曲轴配置上达到3.3mm开环尖端RMSE，较恒曲率基线降低59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机械臂广泛用于医疗场景，精确尖端位置估计对导航和器械定位至关重要。但患者解剖和手术设置导致任务相关的未知轴配置，同时摩擦、松弛和柔顺性引入迟滞，使尖端估计困难。

Method: 首先采用初始多向扫掠策略校准几何导管模型骨干，并将电机角度与驱动扭矩响应编码为轴配置上下文向量。后续运动中，该上下文条件化GRU，仅依赖电机测量预测任务空间残差以校正骨干模型。上下文在当前轴配置下保持固定，而循环状态捕获不断演变的驱动历史。

Result: 在四根一次性心内超声导管和16种弯曲轴配置上，方法实现3.3mm开环尖端RMSE，相对恒曲率基线降低59%。

Conclusion: 该方法无需直接轴配置传感即可实现精确三维尖端估计，对未知轴配置和迟滞具有鲁棒性，验证了电机历史条件化残差学习在腱驱动连续体机器人导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Tendon-driven continuum manipulators are widely used in medical applications, where accurate tip-position estimation is essential for precise navigation and instrument positioning. However, patient anatomy and procedural setup impose task-dependent unknown shaft configurations, while friction, slack, and compliance introduce hysteresis, making tip estimation challenging. This paper presents a motor-history-conditioned gated recurrent unit (GRU) residual estimator for three-dimensional catheter tip estimation without direct shaft-configuration sensing. First, an initial multidirectional sweep strategy is applied to calibrate a geometric catheter model backbone, and encode the motor-angle and drive-torque response into a shaft-configuration context vector. During subsequent motion, the context conditions a GRU that predicts a task-space residual correcting this backbone, relying on motor measurements alone. The context remains fixed for the current shaft configuration, while the recurrent state captures the evolving actuation history. Across four disposable intra-cardiac echocardiography catheters and 16 bent shaft configurations, the method achieves 3.3mm open-loop tip RMSE, a 59% reduction relative to the constant-curvature baseline.

</details>


### [28] [RoboTwin-Phys: Do WAMs and VLAs Understand the Physical World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26292)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Feng Ye,Mingjia Yang,Zhihong Chen,Mingkang Xiang,Xinglin Yao,Yanbin Li,Siwei Ma,Chuanmin Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboTwin-Phys，一个以物理条件多样性为核心维度的机器人操作基准，包含13种物理属性连续变化、5000+专家演示与统一评估协议，并揭示现有WAM/VLA在物理条件变化下鲁棒性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准多关注视觉外观、场景布局等变化，但物理参数通常固定，缺乏质量、摩擦、关节动力学等真实世界物理条件多样性，导致策略的物理鲁棒性未被充分测试。

Method: 构建 RoboTwin-Phys，将物理条件多样性显式纳入机器人操作评估；在物理合理范围内连续变化13种物理属性，提供跨物理操作条件的统一评估设置；发布超过5000条带真值物理参数的专家演示，支持物理属性估计、条件感知建模和物理条件策略训练。

Result: 对代表性WAM和VLA的评估显示明显鲁棒性差距：在现有视觉与布局随机化下仍有效的模型，在物理条件变化下性能可显著下降。

Conclusion: RoboTwin-Phys提供基准、数据和评估协议，用于系统衡量并提升机器人操作对物理条件多样性的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Physical-condition diversity is largely missing from current benchmarks for robot manipulation. While large-scale simulation benchmarks increasingly incorporate variations in object appearance, scene layout, and visual observations, they typically keep the underlying physical parameters fixed. As a result, important sources of real-world variability, such as changes in mass, friction, and joint dynamics, remain largely untested. We introduce RoboTwin-Phys, a physics-diverse benchmark that treats physical-condition diversity as an explicit dimension of robot manipulation evaluation. The benchmark continuously varies 13 physical attributes within physically plausible ranges, providing a unified setting for evaluating policies across diverse physical operating conditions. We further release more than 5,000 expert demonstrations with ground-truth physical parameters, enabling physical-attribute estimation, condition-aware modeling, and physics-conditioned policy training. Evaluations of representative WAMs and VLAs reveal a substantial robustness gap: models that remain effective under existing visual and layout randomization can degrade markedly under changes in physical conditions. RoboTwin-Phys provides the benchmark, data, and evaluation protocol needed to systematically measure and improve robustness to physical-condition diversity in robot manipulation.

</details>


### [29] [Multi-Axis Selective Decoupling Framework for Free-Floating Space Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26267)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多轴选择性解耦框架，仅沿任务关键方向消除动量传递，从而在保持自由漂浮空间机械臂任务空间操作能力的同时，避免传统无反应操作对全旋转轴的过度约束和工作空间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由漂浮空间机械臂因动力学耦合面临操作挑战；传统无反应操作虽可消除基座扰动，但需对所有旋转轴施加零空间投影，约束过强并大幅减小可用工作空间。

Method: 构建方向约束的子耦合矩阵，形成多轴选择性解耦框架，将零空间约束仅施加于任务关键方向，从而放宽约束、保留其余自由度。

Result: 数值仿真验证：该方法可在目标轴上数学上消除受约束方向扰动，同时保留剩余自由度，机械臂能成功执行任务空间操作。

Conclusion: 该框架为自由漂浮空间机械臂提供了一种选择性无反应/解耦操作策略，在基座扰动抑制与工作空间/自由度保留之间取得更优折中。

Abstract: Free-floating space manipulator systems face operational challenges due to dynamic coupling. While traditional reactionless manipulation eliminates base disturbances, enforcing the null-space projections across all rotational axes imposes excessive constraints on the system and drastically reduces the available workspace. To address this limitation, this work proposes a multi-axis selective decoupling framework that nullifies momentum transfer exclusively along mission-critical directions. By leveraging a directionally constrained sub-coupling matrix, the framework relaxes the null-space constraints. Numerical simulations confirm that this approach mathematically eliminates constrained directional disturbances along the targeted axes while preserving the remaining degrees of freedom, enabling the manipulator to execute task-space operations successfully.

</details>


### [30] [High-Bandwidth Biomimetic Finger for Tactile-Transparent Remote Texture Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26256)
*Shuang Yang,Fuyuan Liu,Yitian Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计了一种复现人指多层机械梯度和指纹形貌的仿生指尖，通过内置高灵敏IMU采集纹理振动用于远程振动触觉渲染，并建立从指尖设计、信号特征到触觉透明度的传递链，比较三种变体以提炼设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人遥操作需要高保真触觉反馈以实现精细操作与关键决策；仿生指尖传感器虽能捕获表面纹理特征，但其设计如何影响渲染反馈的触觉透明度尚不清楚。

Method: 提出一种仿生指尖，复现人指多层机械梯度与指纹形貌，并嵌入高灵敏度IMU捕获纹理诱导振动以进行远程振动触觉渲染；将三种变体与人指尖进行时间域和频谱分析，并开展涵盖三个感知维度的用户研究，建立从指尖设计到信号特征再到触觉透明度的传递链。

Result: 多层机械梯度在信号强度和纹理特征表征上提升最明显；更硬的皮肤层会放大振动强度但牺牲特征表征。不同感知维度依赖不同信号属性：粗糙度依赖能量标度，颗粒感依赖频谱形状，重复性依赖特征峰表征。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了指尖设计对触觉透明度的影响机制，并为机器人遥操作中面向特定应用的仿生指尖设计提供了原则。

Abstract: High-fidelity tactile feedback is essential for robotic teleoperation, enabling precise manipulation and critical decision-making. While biomimetic fingertip sensors can capture surface-texture features, how their design shapes the tactile transparency of rendered feedback remains poorly understood. This paper presents a biomimetic fingertip replicating the human finger's multilayer mechanical gradient and fingerprint morphology, with an embedded high-sensitivity inertial measurement unit capturing texture-induced vibrations for remote vibrotactile rendering. Three variants are compared with the human fingertip through temporal and spectral analyses and a user study spanning three perceptual dimensions, establishing a transmission chain from fingertip design through signal characteristics to tactile transparency. The multilayer mechanical gradient yields the clearest improvements in signal intensity and texture-feature representation, whereas a stiffer skin layer amplifies vibration intensity at the expense of feature representation. The perceptual dimensions draw on distinct signal attributes: roughness on energy scaling, granularity on spectral shape, and repetitivity on characteristic-peak representation. These findings offer design principles for application-specific fingertips in robotic teleoperation.

</details>


### [31] [Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects Using Model Predictive Path Integral Control with Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26238)
*Lukas Zeh,Johannes Meiwaldt,Zexu Zhou,Armin Lechler,Alexander Verl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于机器学习的最优控制方法，用biLSTM学习DLO动力学，并采用MPPI模型预测控制，在仿真与实验中实现准确高效的DLO操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（DLO）如线缆具有无限自由度和非线性动力学，其操作对自动化构成重大挑战。

Method: 方法分为建模与控制两部分：建模采用双向长短期记忆网络（biLSTM），使用MuJoCo物理引擎生成的合成数据训练；控制采用基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的模型预测控制策略。

Result: 通过仿真和实验评估，结果证明所提方法能够实现准确且高效的DLO操作。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于biLSTM建模与MPPI控制的方法对DLO操作有效，可达到准确、高效的操作性能。

Abstract: The manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as cables poses a significant challenge for automation due to their infinite degrees of freedom and non-linear dynamics. In this paper we present a machine learning based optimal control approach for the manipulation of DLOs. This approach is divided into two main components: modeling and control. For modeling the dynamics of the DLO, we propose a learning based approach using a bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (biLSTM) network. The biLSTM network is trained on synthetic data generated by the MuJoCo physics engine. For manipulating the DLO, a model predictive control strategy that employs Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is selected. The proposed approach is evaluated through simulation and experiments. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in achieving accurate and efficient manipulation of DLOs.

</details>


### [32] [Silent Sabotage: Internal State Triggered Backdoor Attacks on LLM-Powered Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26184)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Shivayogi Akki,Tan Chen,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究LLM驱动机器人系统中的基于历史的后门攻击：攻击者通过篡改指令植入隐蔽后门，该后门由机器人自身过去动作的罕见序列触发，而非外部刺激；正常运行时保持休眠并维持效用，激活后可诱导停止或碰撞等恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后门研究主要关注由特定词语、视觉对象或环境状态等外部刺激触发的攻击，忽视了触发器内嵌于智能体自身运行逻辑中的更隐蔽漏洞；随着LLM融入机器人控制，需要探究此类内部状态相关安全风险。

Method: 作者提出并开展面向LLM驱动机器人控制器的基于历史的后门攻击研究；通过操纵指令向LLM控制器植入隐蔽后门，触发器为机器人自身过去动作的特定稀有序列；在多种机器人和LLM的仿真环境中进行实验，评估攻击有效性、隐蔽性及对正常任务效用的影响。

Result: 实验表明，该基于历史的后门攻击高度有效，攻击成功率接近完美，同时极难被检测；后门在正常操作期间保持休眠，不显著损害机器人效用，激活后可导致完全停止或碰撞等恶意行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示自主系统中一个关键且此前未被解决的漏洞，表明安全防护必须考虑智能体的内部状态，尤其是历史动作序列等内部触发条件，并凸显针对LLM机器人控制系统的后门检测与防御的紧迫性。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into robotic control systems is enabling a new generation of autonomous agents capable of complex reasoning and planning. While this paradigm shift accelerates progress, it also introduces novel security risks that remain largely unexplored. Current research into LLM backdoors has focused on attacks triggered by external stimuli, such as specific words, visual objects, or environmental states. These attacks, while potent, overlook a more insidious class of vulnerability where the trigger is internal to the agent's own operational logic. This paper presents the first comprehensive study of history-based backdoor attacks on LLM-powered robotic systems. We demonstrate that an attacker can embed a stealthy backdoor into an LLM-based robot controller by manipulating its instructions. This backdoor is triggered not by an external cue, but by a specific, rare sequence of the robot's own past actions. It remains dormant during normal operation, preserving the robot's utility, but can be activated to induce a malicious behavior, such as a complete stop or a collision. Our experiments, conducted in a simulated environment with a variety of robots and LLMs, show that this history-based attack is highly effective, achieving a near-perfect attack success rate while remaining exceptionally difficult to detect. These findings reveal a critical and previously unaddressed vulnerability in autonomous systems and underscore the urgent need for security measures that account for an agent's internal state.

</details>

*Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出面向目标的智能体AI架构，通过情境对话帮助非专家完成机器人硬件维修任务；20人实验中19人完成（95%），系统被认为有帮助且有能力，但物理交互相比在线基线显著降低社会临场感、信任与能力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适机器人系统常缺乏传统视觉界面，因此自然语言交互对其维护与维修至关重要；研究旨在让非专家用户通过情境对话完成技术维修任务。

Method: 提出目标导向的智能体AI架构，将交互前目标分解、持久状态跟踪、策略性目标管理和实时对话执行分离；在物理硬件维修任务中招募20名参与者进行评估，并与先前在线基线进行比较。

Result: 19名参与者完成任务，完成率95%；参与者评价系统有帮助且有能力，智能体能稳健应对元查询、语码转换等对话转移；与在线基线相比，物理交互显著降低感知社会临场感（p=.0005）以及信任与能力（p=.037），但感知有帮助性仍保持较高。

Conclusion: 该架构能有效支持非专家完成机器人维修任务，但物理交互情境会削弱社会临场感、信任与能力感知；有帮助性维持较高，提示未来设计需关注物理交互中的信任与社会存在。 

Abstract: Ubiquitous robotic systems often lack traditional visual interfaces, making natural language interaction important for maintenance and repair. This paper presents a goal-oriented agentic AI architecture that enables non-expert users to complete technical repair tasks through situated dialogue. The architecture separates pre-interaction goal decomposition, persistent state tracking, strategic goal management, and real-time conversational execution. We evaluated the system in a physical hardware repair task with twenty participants. Nineteen participants completed the task, corresponding to a 95% completion rate. Participants rated the system as helpful and competent, and the agent remained robust to conversational diversions such as meta-queries and code-switching. A comparison with a prior online baseline showed that physical interaction significantly reduced perceived social presence, (p=.0005), and trust and competence, (p=.037), while perceived helpfulness remained high.

</details>


### [34] [StepTrigger: Contact-State-Triggered Backdoor Attacks on VLM-Powered Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26131)
*Jiageng Zhang,Doniyorkhon Obidov,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 StepTrigger：一种面向 VLM 驱动四足机器人的接触状态触发后门攻击，利用 Unitree Go1 在稠密地形行走时产生的足-地压力/接触模式作为触发器，而非语言、视觉或动作历史触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM/VLM 日益作为机器人高层规划器，若其被后门污染，可在多数运行中表现正常，仅在隐藏触发器出现时改变目标选择。现有攻击多依赖语言提示、相机可见物体、场景语义或历史动作序列，尚未覆盖本体感觉与接触通道这一新型后门面。

Method: StepTrigger 从多模态机器人状态中学习选择性后门策略：将良性运动中的偶发压力事件作为良性样本，将 Unitree Go1 穿过稠密地形片时产生的足-地接触模式作为投毒样本。触发器不是提示 token 或可见标记，而是压力与接触模式。通过选择性策略避免把所有压力异常误判为触发器。

Result: 在分层离线评估中，训练后的规划器实现：98.75% 干净行为保持、92.50% 假触发拒绝、76.25% 真触发激活、89.17% 总体解析行为准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，本体感觉与接触通道存在一个未被仅关注语言、视觉或动作历史的防御机制所覆盖的后门攻击面。

Abstract: Large language models and vision-language models are increasingly used as high-level planners in robotic systems, using task goals and sensor summaries to select navigation or manipulation actions. This creates a new backdoor surface: a compromised planner can behave normally in most runs, yet change its target selection when a hidden trigger is present. Prior attacks on LLM-powered or embodied agents mainly rely on triggers that appear in language, camera-visible objects, scene semantics, or specific sequences of past actions. This paper presents StepTrigger, a contact-state-triggered backdoor attack for VLM-powered legged robots. The trigger is not a prompt token or a visible marker. It is produced by pressure and foot-ground contact patterns that arise when a Unitree Go1 quadruped walks across a dense terrain patch. Unlike conventional visual or textual triggers, contact signals are inherently noisy and may also arise during benign locomotion. To avoid treating every pressure anomaly as a trigger, StepTrigger learns a selective backdoor policy from multimodal robot state, using incidental pressure events as benign examples and dense-patch contacts as poisoned examples. In a stratified offline evaluation, the trained planner achieved 98.75% clean behavior preservation, 92.50% false-trigger rejection, 76.25% true-trigger activation, and 89.17% overall parsed behavior accuracy. These results reveal a backdoor surface in proprioceptive and contact channels that is not captured by defenses focused only on language, vision, or action history.

</details>


### [35] [Design and Implementation of an Ultra-Low-Cost Wall-Climbing Robot for Infrastructure Crack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26130)
*Mrinmoy Modak,Supreyo Chakravorty Pretom,Shourv Tarafder,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种低成本自动化裂缝检测机器人，结合CNN、EfficientNet-B0与YOLOv8，用于混凝土表面裂缝的实时识别与定位，并配套构建了裂缝图像数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂缝检测对建筑和基础设施的安全与寿命至关重要，但发展中国家常受预算、人力与安全风险限制，亟需可负担、可扩展的自动化检测方案。

Method: 系统采用两阶段流程：第一阶段用YOLOv8从视频帧中检测并定位墙体区域，裁剪边界框；第二阶段在裁剪区域上用CNN或EfficientNet-B0等模型进行裂缝分类/检测。硬件包括风扇负压吸附、四轮滑移转向、ESP32-CAM实时图像采集、轻量3D打印底盘，并采用漏斗形机身增强负压；同时开发了支持智能手机控制的轻量Web应用。

Result: YOLOv8提供实时裂缝定位，CNN与EfficientNet-B0保证较高精度和召回率；机器人可在墙面和天花板移动并采集图像，漏斗形设计使占空比降低44%，显著降低功耗；整套系统总成本约25美元。

Conclusion: 该系统将低成本硬件与深度学习流水线结合，提供可扩展、高效且易获取的实时基础设施裂缝检测方案，尤其适合预算受限的发展中地区。

Abstract: Crack detection is a crucial process to ensure the safety and longevity of buildings and other infrastructure. In this paper, we developed a low-cost, automated crack detection robot that leverages CNN, EfficientNet-B0, and YOLOv8 for efficient identification of cracks in concrete surfaces with a curated crack image dataset introduced to support training and evaluation. YOLOv8's real-time object detection enhances crack localization, while CNN and EfficientNet-B0 provide binary classification, ensuring high precision and recall. The system consists of two stages. In the first stage, YOLOv8 detects and localizes wall regions from the video frame, and the bounding boxes are cropped. The second stage performs crack detection using one of three models by analyzing the cropped regions. Cost-effective approaches are also taken for robot design. The robot features a fan-based negative pressure adhesion system, a 4-wheeled skid-steering drive, and an ESP32-CAM for real-time image capture. Its lightweight 3D-printed chassis ensures stability, allowing it to navigate both walls and ceilings while capturing images for crack analysis. Unlike conventional wall-climbing robot designs, this robot incorporates a funnel-shaped body that enhances negative pressure generation and achieves a 44% reduction in duty cycle, significantly lowering power consumption.
