By combining low-cost hardware with a deep learning pipeline, our system provides a scalable, efficient, and accessible solution for real-time infrastructure inspection at an approximate total cost of $25, with a lightweight web application enabling smartphone-based control. This affordability makes the system more suitable for the developing world, where infrastructure inspection is often limited by budget constraints, labor intensity, and safety risks.

</details>


### [36] [GDLAM: Group-Disentangled Latent Action Model for Highly Disentangled Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26118)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiawei Li,Jiale Zhang,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Maowei Hu,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出组解缠潜在动作模型GDLAM，将潜在动作码按构造分解为N个独立组，并通过信息几何目标增强组间因果区分性，从而提升世界模型可控性与VLA策略泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型从无动作视频中自监督学习动作表示时，将相机运动、物体动力学和交互事件等异构视觉变化源压缩到单一潜在向量中，导致表示纠缠、语义结构不足，进而限制世界模型可控性和VLA策略泛化。

Method: GDLAM按构造将潜在动作码因子化为N组，每组具有独立变分瓶颈与空间门控路由通路，并采用信息几何目标训练：互斥性、组稀疏性与门稀疏性、静态-动态正交性，使各组不仅去相关，而且在因果上相互区分。

Result: 干预任一组仅改变该组而不影响其他组；在Modularity、MIG和DCI等无标签解缠指标上大幅优于强非结构化LAM；在三种互信息估计器和线性探针下，分组码在分布内与分布外均比单体基线更具动作信息。作为可复用预训练表示，GDLAM预训练的世界模型在展开保真度和动作跟随能力上优于SOTA基线；用于VLA策略预训练时，在多个仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务上显著提升成功率。

Conclusion: GDLAM通过组解缠潜在动作表示缓解了异构视觉变化源纠缠问题，在保持甚至增强动作信息的同时提升解缠质量，并可作为可复用预训练表示有效迁移至世界建模与VLA策略学习。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action-related representations from action-free videos via self-supervised future prediction, offering a scalable paradigm for embodied intelligence pretraining. However, existing LAMs collapse heterogeneous sources of visual change, including camera motion, object dynamics, and interaction events, into a single latent vector, resulting in entangled representations with limited semantic structure and consequently restricting world model controllability and VLA policy generalization. We introduce the Group-Disentangled Latent Action Model (GDLAM), a latent action model whose code is factorized by construction into N groups, each with an independent variational bottleneck and a spatially gated routing pathway, and trained with a set of information-geometric objectives: mutual exclusivity, group and gate sparsity, and static-dynamic orthogonality, that make the groups mutually causally distinct rather than merely decorrelated. Quantitatively, intervening on any single group changes only that group and leaves the others intact, and GDLAM improves label-free disentanglement metrics, including Modularity, MIG, and DCI, by wide margins over a strong unstructured LAM. Notably, this factorization is not at the expense of action information: across three mutual-information estimators and a linear probe, the grouped code is more informative than monolithic baselines both in- and out-of-distribution. As supporting evidence that the disentangled code is a reusable pretraining currency, we further transfer it to two downstream regimes: (1) World Modeling: World models pretrained with GDLAM achieve superior rollout fidelity and action-following capability compared with SOTA baselines. (2) VLA Policies: Pretraining with GDLAM substantially improves task success rates over previous methods across multiple simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks

</details>


### [37] [Acoustic Ellipses: Bio-Inspired Omnidirectional Echolocation in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems using Frequency Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26085)
*Petras Swissler,Lindsay Burke,Julia Hyland Bruno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仿鸟鸣的协作式障碍物感知与建图方法：固定声源发射调频 chirp，移动听者接收直射与反射信号，利用二者间的频率差生成候选反射椭圆，再经二维累积滤波定位障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受鸟类飞行与鸣叫启发，旨在为群体机器人系统提供一种低成本的环境建图与障碍物识别方案。

Method: 固定源代理发射频率调制 chirp；一个或多个听者代理在运动中观测直射与反射信号。利用直射与反射 chirp 信号之间的“频率间隙”定义候选反射椭圆，并将其输入二维累积滤波器，以识别潜在反射密度最高的位置。

Result: 通过定制高效音频仿真器验证：单听者场景下考察障碍物数量、采样率和路径曲率对障碍物识别精度的影响；双听者场景下比较不同协作运动策略；并在户外公园使用现成硬件进行实地实验，证明能够识别频率间隙。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法可作为群体机器人系统中低成本环境建图的基础。

Abstract: Inspired by the flight and song of birds, we propose an approach that enables cooperating agents to effectively identify obstacles in their environment by having a stationary source agent emit a frequency-modulated chirp while one or more listener agents observe the direct and reflected signals while in motion. We present a novel mapping approach that exploits the ``frequency gap'' between direct and reflected chirp signals to define candidate reflection ellipses, which are then fed into a 2D accumulation filter to identify locations with the highest density of potential reflections. We demonstrate this work first with simulation results derived from an efficient, bespoke audio simulator, examining the effect of obstacle count, sampling rate, and path curvature on the ability to accurately identify environmental obstacles for a one-listener scenario. We then examine different cooperative motion strategies for two-listener configurations. Finally, we validate the real-world viability of this approach through field experiments in an outdoor park setting to demonstrate the ability to identify frequency gaps using off-the-shelf hardware. Our results provide a foundation for a low-cost approach to environmental mapping in swarm robotic systems.

</details>


### [38] [Vision-Language Models as copilots for Autonomous UAV Navigation: Analysis of Latency and Reliability in Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26084)
*Hiago Sodre,Sebastian Barcelona,Vincent Sandin,Pablo Moraes,Ahilen Mazondo,Igor Nunes,William Moraes,André Kelbouscas,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于GPS拒止环境下无人机的FSM-VLM混合控制架构：确定性有限状态机负责底层物理控制，异步VLM副驾驶负责高层语义寻路。在SITL仿真中评估三种不同参数规模的模型，并在正常与退化场景下区分格式级语法错误和逻辑级语义幻觉。研究表明，模型参数规模而非纯推理延迟，是VLM安全且兼容地集成到自主飞行中的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM为无人机带来语义推理能力，但实时闭环导航不仅要求低推理延迟，还要求服从结构化飞行指令。因此需要解决GPS-free环境中VLM集成时的安全性、兼容性与实时控制问题。

Method: 设计混合FSM-VLM控制架构，将确定性FSM用于低层物理控制，将异步VLM用于高层语义路径规划。在软件在环（SITL）仿真中评估三种不同参数规模的模型，并设置正常与退化场景，以隔离并度量格式层面的语法错误与逻辑层面的语义幻觉。

Result: 评估表明，不同参数规模模型在正常与退化场景下的语法错误和语义幻觉存在差异；参数规模而非纯延迟是影响VLM安全、兼容集成到自主飞行中的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 混合FSM-VLM架构为GPS-free环境下无人机实时闭环语义导航提供了可行方案，但VLM的安全集成更依赖模型参数容量与语义可靠性，而非仅优化推理延迟。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offers unprecedented semantic reasoning capabilities. However, real-time closed-loop navigation requires not only low inference latency but also obedience to structured flight commands. This paper proposes a hybrid FSM-VLM control architecture for UAVs in GPS-free environments. The system combines a deterministic Finite State Machine (FSM) for low-level physical control with an asynchronous VLM copilot for high-level semantic pathfinding. We evaluate three models with different parameter scales in a Software-In-The-Loop (SITL) simulation. The framework isolates and measures syntax errors at the format level versus semantic hallucinations at the logic level in a normal and degraded scenario. This study demonstrates that parameter scaling, and not pure latency, remains the primary bottleneck for the safe and compatible integration of VLM into autonomous flights.

</details>


### [39] [Situation Aware Locomotion for Dual Mobile Cobots in Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26083)
*William Moraes,Igor Nunes,Ahilen Mazondo,Sebastian Barcelona,Hiago Sodre,Pablo Moraes,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向共享工业环境中两台移动协作机器人的情境感知运动框架，通过感知、理解与预测支持运动决策；仿真结果显示其任务成功率100%，优于两个基线各33.3%，并消除共享区冲突与安全停止，保持最大平均最小机器人间距且平均完成时间最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两台移动协作机器人在共享工业环境中安全、高效运动的问题，需综合负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区占用、障碍物状态及机器人间冲突预测等因素。

Method: 将情境感知建模为感知、理解和投影，利用机器人位姿、负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区占用、障碍物状态及预测的机器人间冲突来选择安全运动动作；在匹配4 m×4 m实体测试区的仿真工业场景中实现，并通过多次试验和随机种子与独立基线、固定优先级基线比较。

Result: 所提情境感知方法在所有场景中任务成功率100%，独立基线和固定优先级基线总体成功率均为33.3%；该方法消除了共享区冲突和安全停止，保持了最大的平均最小机器人间距，并实现最低平均完成时间。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区推理和短时冲突预测的情境感知能够改善双机器人的运动性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a situation aware locomotion framework for two mobile collaborative robots operating in shared industrial environments. The proposed method models situation awareness through perception, comprehension, and projection to support locomotion decisions. Robot pose, load state, manipulator state, shared zone occupancy, obstacle state, and predicted inter robot conflict were used to select safe locomotion actions. The framework was implemented in simulation and evaluated in simulated industrial scenarios designed to match a feasible 4 m by 4 m physical test area. The proposed method was compared with two other baselines over multiple trials and randomized seeds. The results show that the situation aware method achieved 100% task success across all scenarios, while the independent and fixed priority baselines each achieved 33.3% overall success. The proposed method eliminated shared zone conflicts and safety stops, maintained the largest average minimum inter robot distance, and completed the tasks with the lowest average completion time. These results indicate that situational awareness can improve the locomotion of dual robots by combining load state, manipulator state, reasoning about the shared zone, and prediction of short-horizon conflicts.

</details>


### [40] [StrataVLA: Hierarchical and Efficient 3D Geometric Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26071)
*Jin Cui,Zhaoyu Pu,Botao Cai,Jun Ye,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StrataVLA 提出一种即插即用的分层几何接地框架，通过冻结几何基础模型与层特异性几何适配器，将几何证据注入 VLA 骨干网络，并结合任务感知路由与 LRU 缓存降低推理开销，在多个基准和真实操作任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽继承大规模视觉-语言预训练的强语义先验，但因缺乏充足的三维空间感知而限制机器人操作能力。现有方法要么依赖显式深度或点云输入，要么仅在训练时压缩几何监督，或仅在模型输入/动作专家处注入几何信息，导致视觉-语言骨干网络无法持续获取任务相关空间信息。

Method: 提出 StrataVLA，即插即用的分层几何接地框架。使用冻结的几何基础模型从 RGB 观测中提取共享几何特征；在骨干网络选定深度处设置稀疏、层特异性的 Geometry Adapters，通过交叉注意力检索相关几何证据。为使推理时几何计算可行，进一步结合任务感知路由与 LRU 特征缓存，利用操作任务中的时间冗余。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv 和真实世界操作任务上，StrataVLA 相比强 VLA 基线取得一致增益；在 LIBERO 套件上平均成功率达 98.53%，同时将几何模型调用次数最多减少 88%。

Conclusion: 分层几何注入是实现空间接地机器人控制的有效且高效途径，表明无需持续密集调用几何模型也能显著增强 VLA 模型的三维空间感知与操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit strong semantic priors from large-scale vision-language pretraining, yet remain limited in robotic manipulation by insufficient 3D spatial awareness. Existing approaches either require explicit depth or point-cloud inputs, compress geometry into training-time supervision, or inject it only at the model input or action expert, leaving the vision-language backbone without persistent access to task-relevant spatial information. We introduce StrataVLA, a plug-and-play framework for hierarchical geometric grounding. A frozen geometry foundation model extracts shared geometric features from RGB observations, while sparse, layer-specific Geometry Adapters allow visual representations at selected backbone depths to retrieve relevant geometric evidence through cross-attention. To make inference-time geometry practical, StrataVLA further combines task-aware routing with an LRU feature cache that exploits temporal redundancy during task manipulation. Experiments on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over strong VLA baselines. StrataVLA achieves 98.53% average success on LIBERO suites while reducing geometry-model invocations by up to 88%, establishing hierarchical geometry injection as an effective and efficient way to achieve spatially grounded robotic control.

</details>


### [41] [Towards Intent-Aware Human-Robot Teaming: A Platform for Search-and-Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26051)
*Rohith Prem Maben,Ayesha Jena,Björn Olofsson,Stefan Reitmann,Jacek Malec,Rogier Woltjer,Elin Anna Topp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建交互平台，探索复杂动态环境中人类操作员与异构自主智能体的有效协作，并在搜救任务中验证自主系统对操作员感知、决策与行动的支持程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂动态环境中，人类操作员与异构自主智能体之间的有效协作面临挑战，需要研究操作员行为并支持意图推断与决策。

Method: 开发一个交互平台，用于研究操作员行为，并基于前沿框架支持意图推断和决策；以搜救行动为场景进行演示。

Result: 在搜救操作中展示了自主系统能够在多大程度上支持操作员的感知、决策和行动。

Conclusion: 该平台有助于研究并促进人机异构智能体协作，表明自主系统在搜救等复杂任务中具备支持操作员关键认知与决策过程的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the challenges of enabling effective collaboration between human operators and heterogeneous autonomous agents in complex, dynamic environments by developing an interaction platform that allows study of operator behavior and supports intent inference and decision-making using state-of-the-art frameworks. We demonstrate the extent to which the operator's perception, decisions, and actions could be supported by autonomous systems during search-and-rescue operations with our platform.

</details>


### [42] [Skytopia: Monocular Drone Navigation with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26007)
*Yuhang Zhang,Rangya Zhang,Yujing Shang,Zhuoyuan Yu,Weiying Wang,Steven Yang,Qingsong Yan,Chao Yan,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 skytopia——一种基于动作条件潜在世界模型的单目无人机导航策略：训练时用前向目标预测下一观测表示、逆向目标恢复运动，但推理时丢弃预测器，用单一策略统一支持 point-goal、image-goal 与 goal-free 导航，并在真实无人机上零微调部署成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目前视相机导航缺乏深度与尺度线索。世界模型虽能建模观测随动作的演化，但通常被设计为在部署时执行预测并逐步回灌到动作生成，控制代价高。作者主张策略真正需要的不是预测结果，而是产生该预测所需的表示：飞行中已执行动作几乎解释了观测间的全部变化，因此预测可退化为在已知位移下对静态场景的重投影。

Method: 构建在动作条件潜在世界模型之上、并以 3D 高斯溅射（3D Gaussian Splatting）平台训练的 skytopia 策略。前向目标由意图运动预测下一观测的表示，逆向目标从预测的转移中恢复运动。由于预测不进入动作生成，预测器在部署时被丢弃，从而用同一个策略服务 point-goal、image-goal 和 goal-free 三种导航设定。

Result: 仿真实验中 skytopia 在三种设定下均优于全部基线，成功率分别为 57.8%、66.0% 和 49.0%；丢弃预测器可减少 59.4% 的推理成本。同一策略随后无需微调即部署到实体无人机，并能在室内、开阔户外与林地环境中到达目标。

Conclusion: 策略从世界模型中所需的并非预测输出而是其表示；据此设计的 skytopia 以高效推理实现了跨任务规格的统一单目无人机导航，并具备零微调的实机泛化能力。

Abstract: Monocular drone navigation requires reaching a goal in an unseen environment from a single forward-facing camera, which offers few cues for depth and scale. World models address this by modelling how observations evolve under actions, but they are built to be executed: the prediction is produced at deployment and fed back into action generation at every control step. We argue that what a policy needs from a world model is not the prediction but the representation required to produce it: in flight the executed action explains almost all of the change between observations, so prediction reduces to reprojecting a static scene under a known displacement. We therefore introduce skytopia, a policy built on an action-conditioned latent world model, and the 3D Gaussian Splatting platform on which it is trained. A forward objective predicts the representation of the next observation from the intended motion, and an inverse objective recovers that motion from the predicted transition. Because the prediction never reaches action generation, the predictor is discarded and one policy serves point-goal, image-goal, and goal-free navigation. Simulation experiments show that skytopia outperforms every baseline under all three specifications, attaining 57.8%, 66.0%, and 49.0% success rate, while discarding the predictor removes 59.4% of the inference cost. The same policy is subsequently deployed on a physical drone without fine-tuning and reaches goals in indoor, open outdoor, and woodland environments.

</details>


### [43] [Manipulation with Stability Guarantees: Linear Deformable Objects with Non-negligible Physical Response Grasped at Multiple Location](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26004)
*Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作针对可变形物体操作中普遍忽略动力学的准静态假设，提出一种显式考虑物体动力学、将操作建模为形状调节问题的闭环控制架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数可变形物体操作研究局限于轻量、机械响应可忽略的准静态场景，无法覆盖软管、管道、线束等动力学不可忽略且实际重要的物体。

Method: 构建基于离散应变参数化的线性可变形物体全动力学模型；将驱动坐标推广至SE(3)，形成结构化且本质欠驱动的控制架构；设计非线性反馈策略，并给出稳态收敛到期望构型的显式条件；通过多点施加力与力矩实现形状调节闭环控制。

Result: 在代表性操作任务的广泛仿真中，基于模型的表述带来性能提升；并通过具有在线形状估计的实时闭环实现进行实验验证，证明其实际可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了准静态限制，为动力学不可忽略的可变形物体操作提供了结构化、可收敛且实验可行的闭环形状控制方案。

Abstract: Most research on the manipulation of deformable objects focuses on lightweight systems with negligible mechanical response, effectively restricting attention to quasi-static regimes. This assumption excludes a broad class of practically relevant objects, such as hoses, pipes, and wiring harnesses, whose dynamics cannot be ignored during manipulation. In this work, we address this limitation by introducing a closed-loop control architecture that explicitly accounts for object dynamics and recasts manipulation as a shape-regulation problem. Control is achieved by modulating forces and torques applied at multiple fixed points along the object. This approach builds on three methodological contributions: a fully dynamic model of linear deformable objects based on discrete strain parameterizations; an extension of the notion of actuation coordinates to SE(3), yielding a structured and inherently underactuated control architecture; and nonlinear feedback strategies providing explicit conditions for steady-state convergence to desired configurations. Extensive simulations on representative manipulation tasks demonstrate the performance gains enabled by the proposed modelbased formulation. We finally validate the approach experimentally through a real-time closed-loop implementation with online shape estimation, confirming its practical feasibility and effectiveness

</details>


### [44] [Safety-Constrained Model Predictive Control for an Omnidirectional Walking Assistive Robot Using Control Barrier Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25994)
*Andrea Fortuna,Marta Lorenzini,Elisa Motta,Alberto Ranavolo,Elena De Momi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将控制屏障函数（CBF）融入模型预测控制（MPC）的全向步行辅助机器人I-WANDER控制框架，实验表明其可降低能耗与碰撞并保持运动平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为运动障碍者提供安全有效的移动辅助对恢复独立性和提升生活质量至关重要；机器人步行辅助设备需在保证用户安全与支撑的同时实现物理顺应的人机交互。

Method: 针对全向步行辅助机器人I-WANDER，提出CBF-MPC控制框架，将CBF嵌入MPC以显式强制避碰安全约束，并优化能效与人机协作平滑性。实验招募12名健康参与者完成两项任务：U形走廊结构化导航与蒙眼单障碍避障，对比CB-MPC与可变导纳控制器（AC）。

Result: CB-MPC显著降低能耗和机械功（p < 0.01），且不牺牲运动平滑性，同时减少障碍碰撞次数。

Conclusion: 该控制架构在提升机器人步行辅助的安全性和效率方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Providing safe and effective mobility assistance plays a crucial role in restoring independence and enhancing the quality of life for individuals with motor impairments. In this context, robotic walking assistive devices have recently emerged as promising solutions to provide physically compliant interaction while ensuring user safety and support. This paper presents a novel control framework for an omnidirectional Walking Assistive Robot (I-WANDER) that integrates a Control Barrier Function (CBF) formulation into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) scheme to explicitly enforce collision-avoidance safety constraints while optimizing for energy efficiency and smooth human-robot collaboration. The method was experimentally evaluated with 12 healthy participants performing two different walking tasks using both the proposed CBF-based MPC controller (CB-MPC) and a variable admittance controller (AC). The first task involved structured navigation through a U-shaped corridor, whereas the second consisted of a single-obstacle avoidance task performed blindfolded to ensure the obstacle was unexpected. Comparative results show that the CB-MPC architecture significantly reduces energy consumption and mechanical work (p < 0.01) without compromising motion smoothness, while also decreasing the number of obstacle collisions. Overall, the findings highlight the potential of the proposed control architecture to enhance both safety and efficiency in robotic walking assistance.

</details>


### [45] [Predict Before You Step: Auditable Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance under Sparse Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25969)
*Yuhui Mao,Fen Liu,Shenghai Yuan,Tianxin Hu,Ruimeng Liu,Rong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOOP 是一种将稀疏路点引导与冻结运动控制器在 50 Hz 连接的局部避障策略；它用循环预测器预测 1 s 未来占据地图，并据此选择速度，在仿真和 Unitree Go2 实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在稀疏路点引导下需依赖部分且快速变化的 LiDAR 观测避开移动障碍物，现有反应式策略缺乏对未来占据的显式预测，难以在高速正面相遇等场景稳定避障。

Method: LOOP 从占据与自速度历史输入，使用循环预测器通过学到的流和可见性门对当前地图进行形变，预测未来 1 s 占据；再由地图派生特征和几何风险估计进行速度选择，形成可检查、可替换预测的显式接口，并以 50 Hz 连接稀疏路点与冻结运动控制器。

Result: 在 encounter-synchronised Isaac Lab 评估中，LOOP 在障碍速度 2.5–3.2 m/s 的正面相遇成功率为 57.1%，比重新训练的反应式基线高 8.2 个百分点；与无 rollout 的 BEV 策略相比，预测分支带来较小且依赖场景的增益，包括改善横穿成功率和在最高正面速度下降低跨训练种子方差；适配器在 Unitree Go2 上每步 14.5 ms，16 次真实横穿试验全部无碰撞。

Conclusion: LOOP 表明，通过显式未来占据预测连接稀疏路点与冻结运动控制器，可提升动态避障性能并实现机载部署；但预测带来的收益具有场景依赖性，显式接口为检查和替换预测提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Legged robots under sparse waypoint guidance must avoid moving obstacles using partial, rapidly changing LiDAR observations. We present LOOP (Latent-recurrent Occupancy rollOut Policy), a local avoidance policy that connects sparse waypoint guidance to a frozen locomotion controller at 50 Hz. From occupancy and ego-velocity histories, a recurrent predictor forecasts future occupancy over a 1 s horizon by warping the current map with learned flow and visibility gates. These maps guide velocity selection through map-derived features and geometric risk estimates, providing an explicit interface for inspecting and replacing predictions. In encounter-synchronised Isaac Lab evaluations, LOOP achieves 57.1% head-on success at obstacle speeds of 2.5-3.2 m/s, exceeding a retrained reactive baseline by 8.2 percentage points. Comparisons with a rollout-free BEV policy show smaller, scenario-dependent gains from the prediction branch, including improved crossing success and reduced variability across training seeds at the highest head-on speeds. The adapter runs onboard a Unitree Go2 in 14.5 ms per step and completes all 16 real-world crossing trials without collision, demonstrating deployment feasibility.

</details>


### [46] [An Action Is Worth One Patch: Unified World-Action Modeling with PatchWAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25961)
*Tianheng Wang,Zhou Xie,Heng Jia,Jianhua Xu,Tong Zhang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PatchWAM，将连续动作通过固定映射Action-as-Patch表示为视觉patch，使单一生成模型同时完成视觉预测与动作生成，无需独立动作头或动作专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视觉模型可用于学习物理动态表示，但扩展到连续控制时通常需要额外可训练动作头或独立动作专家，以弥合低维状态与高维视觉表示之间的差距。本文质疑视觉预测与动作生成是否必须分离，并探索视觉主干已有能力能否在兼容表示下直接支持控制。

Method: PatchWAM将连续动作视为另一类patch，通过固定映射Action-as-Patch写入模型。该单一模型既能预测机器人应如何运动，也能预测场景未来外观，使视觉预测与动作生成统一为同一生成过程，不引入专用动作头或独立动作专家。

Result: 在子采样训练窗口实验中，该方法相比匹配的双专家控制取得提升；在全数据并加入增强演示的设置下，LIBERO-Plus达到91.8%成功率，RoboTwin 2.0达到96.12%成功率。

Conclusion: 能力不必在缺失处新增，而可被继承；扩展生成式主干的约束在于新信号写入的接口，而非模型建模该信号的能力。

Abstract: Generative visual models offer a foundation for learning representations of physical dynamics, yet their extension to continuous control raises a fundamental question: do visual prediction and action generation require separate computational pathways? Existing approaches usually introduce trainable action heads or separate action experts to bridge low-dimensional states and high-dimensional visual representations. In this work, we explore whether the visual backbone's existing capacity can also support control when actions are expressed in a compatible representation. Thus, we introduce PatchWAM (Patch World-Action Model), which treats continuous actions as another type of patch through a fixed mapping called Action-as-Patch. This allows a single model to predict both how the robot should move and what the scene may look like afterward. Visual prediction and action generation become parts of the same generative process, without a dedicated action head or separate action expert. Experiments with subsampled training windows show gains over a matched dual-expert control, while benchmark evaluations reach 91.8% success rate on LIBERO-Plus and 96.12% on RoboTwin 2.0 in a full-data setting with additional augmented demonstrations. More broadly, the result suggests that capability need not be added where it can be inherited: the constraint on extending a generative backbone is the interface a new signal is written in, not the capacity to model it.

</details>


### [47] [Destination Support Restoration for Finite-Set Multimodal Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25942)
*Fengrui Liu,Jiajun Peng,Duo Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Destination Support Restoration（DSR），一种因果后选择算子，用于修复行人轨迹预测中有限假设集的目的地支持分配，无需重训练宿主预测器或增大维护集合大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在行人周围运行时通常基于有限的预测未来集合进行推理；反复在线更新会使有限预测预算集中于主导目的地，导致合理替代目的地表示不足或缺失，从而影响下游决策。

Method: DSR 在修复步中根据观测前缀评估临时的按目的地分层候选库，将候选证据转换为整数目标计数，保护活跃模式的代表，并将冗余剩余假设重新分配给不足模式。维护和返回集合始终保持恰好 N 个假设，DSR 最多替换 ceil(ρN) 个条目。受保护代表保留当前类别支持；在 lineage-aware 粒子滤波中还保留存活的 resampling 祖先。每次替换将分配不匹配相对证据驱动的目标减少 1。

Result: 在完整的 3,719 条轨迹 Edinburgh 协议、三个随机种子上，DSR 在 N=64 时将 MIF 加权 ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 13.36% 和 13.30%。与 CLiFF、PPT、causal GDTS、Social Informer 和 PECNet 的成对集成在每一评估对中均改善两项指标。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当固定假设集作为下游系统接口时，有限集支持分配是一个有用的预测侧控制点。

Abstract: Robots operating around pedestrians often reason over a finite set of predicted human futures. Repeated online updates can concentrate this limited prediction budget on dominant destinations and leave plausible alternatives underrepresented or absent, removing those alternatives from the finite representation available to downstream decision making. We introduce Destination Support Restoration (DSR), a causal post-selection operator that repairs destination support without retraining the host predictor or increasing the maintained set size. At a repair step, DSR evaluates a temporary destination-stratified candidate bank from the observed prefix, converts candidate evidence into integer target counts, protects representatives of active modes, and reallocates redundant surplus hypotheses to deficient modes. The maintained and returned sets retain exactly $N$ hypotheses, and DSR replaces at most $\lceil\rho N\rceil$ entries. Protected representatives preserve current categorical support; lineage-aware particle filters also preserve surviving resampling ancestors. Each replacement reduces the allocation mismatch to the evidence-driven target by one. On the complete 3,719-trajectory Edinburgh protocol over three seeds, DSR reduces MIF weighted ADE and FDE by 13.36% and 13.30% at $N=64$. Paired integrations with CLiFF, PPT, causal GDTS, Social Informer, and PECNet improve both metrics in every evaluated pair. These results show that finite-set support allocation is a useful prediction-side control point when a fixed hypothesis set serves as the interface to downstream systems.

</details>


### [48] [Unsigned Distance Maps on 2D Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25932)
*Ricardo B. Sousa,Giorgio Grisetti,Héber Miguel Sobreira,Carlos André Silva,António Paulo Moreira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于无符号距离图预计算的2D点云配准方法，以O(1)查表替代ICP逐次最近邻搜索，并在SE(2)流形上用Gauss-Newton求解点到点与点到面误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D点云配准广泛用于移动机器人激光里程计与SLAM。ICP虽常用，但每次迭代都需通过最近邻搜索重新计算对应关系；无对应方法则多聚焦于scan-to-map对齐。本文旨在利用预计算距离场避免重复搜索，同时保持配准精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 在离散网格上预计算到最近参考点的欧氏距离及其空间导数，即无符号距离图，从而把逐迭代对应搜索替换为O(1)查询。随后在SE(2)流形上推导点到点与点到面误差形式，并采用Gauss-Newton优化求解。

Result: 在合成基准和真实IILABS 3D数据集上，预计算的点到点变体优于其解析对应版本；与点到面形式相比，其激光里程计漂移具有竞争力，原因是预计算梯度在传感器噪声存在时可正则化对应关系。

Conclusion: 预计算距离图可有效替代ICP中逐迭代的最近邻搜索，实现高效的2D点云配准；其中点到点预计算方案在噪声条件下表现稳健，并取得有竞争力的激光里程计精度。

Abstract: 2D point cloud registration arises in laser odometry and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) for mobile robots. Iterative Closest Point (ICP) is one of the most widely used approaches. Still, its iterative procedure recomputes correspondences via nearest-neighbor search at every iteration, whereas correspondence-free alternatives focus on scan-to-map alignment. This paper proposes a 2D point cloud registration approach based on unsigned distance maps, precomputing the Euclidean distance to the nearest reference point, along with its spatial derivatives, over a discrete grid, replacing the per-iteration search with O(1) lookups. Moreover, point-to-point and point-to-plane error formulations are derived on the SE(2) manifold and solved via Gauss-Newton optimization. On a synthetic benchmark and the real-world IILABS 3D dataset, the precomputed point-to-point variant outperforms its analytical counterparts, achieving competitive laser-odometry drift compared to point-to-plane formulations, as the precomputed gradient regularizes correspondences in the presence of sensor noise.

</details>


### [49] [Vision-based Underwater Formation Control With Input Saturations via Barrier Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25917)
*Nicola De Carli,João Zenário,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无通信、基于视觉的全驱动水下机器人编队控制框架，处理感知约束、避碰要求和输入饱和，并通过Gazebo软件在环仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下通信受限或不可靠，视觉编队控制需在感知范围、避碰安全及执行器饱和等约束下保证可行性与安全性，因此需要无通信且能应对约束的控制方法。

Method: 采用重新中心化障碍Lyapunov函数编码感知与避碰约束；利用命令滤波反步法将设计扩展至二阶载体动力学；通过二次规划显式处理执行器限幅；设置保守感知域以提供物理极限裕度，并在必要时自适应放松，允许临时违反保守边界。

Result: 在Gazebo中通过逼真的软件在环（SITL）仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无通信条件下实现满足感知、避碰与饱和约束的水下机器人编队控制，且保守约束可自适应放松，以平衡安全裕度与控制可行性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a communication-free framework for vision-based formation control of fully actuated underwater robots subject to sensing constraints, collision-avoidance requirements, and input saturations. Recentered barrier Lyapunov functions encode sensing and collision-avoidance constraints, while command-filtered backstepping extends the design to the second-order vehicle dynamics. The resulting control objective is enforced through a quadratic program that explicitly accounts for actuator limits. Conservative sensing domains provide margins from the physical limits and are adaptively relaxed when necessary, allowing temporary violation of the conservative bounds. The proposed approach is validated through realistic Software-in-the-Loop (SITL) simulations in Gazebo.

</details>


### [50] [Control Barrier Functions for Safe Free-Flying Robotic Spacecraft Operations in Tumbling Target Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25905)
*Alexander Meinert,Peter Stadler,Niklas Baldauf,Alen Turnwald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化控制障碍函数（CBF）框架，用于自由飞行机器人航天器在翻滚目标捕获中的安全操作，并通过高保真仿真验证其效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受ESA安全近距离操作指南驱动，需要将安全区与操作要求转化为可执行约束，以支持系统级安全并满足翻滚目标捕获中的多约束、模块化控制需求。

Method: 将13自由度系统分解为平移、姿态和机械臂子系统，各配置安全滤波器，并在轻量二次规划中最小修改标称控制输入；约束涵盖锥形接近走廊、碰撞避免区、姿态视线指向、角速度限制、关节限制、连杆-基座避碰和执行器约束；子系统动态耦合通过将上游安全控制指令视为下游滤波器已知互联输入来处理，兼顾模块化与系统级安全。

Result: 在在轨服务场景中验证，包括最终接近、角速度同步和翻滚目标抓取，使用高保真天体动力学仿真器Basilisk；蒙特卡洛仿真表明，在不同翻滚速率下具有运行时效率与操作安全性。

Conclusion: 该模块化CBF框架能够为自由飞行机器人航天器的翻滚目标捕获提供安全保证，并有效处理子系统耦合，实现运行时高效且支持系统级安全的控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a modular control barrier function (CBF) framework for safe free-flying robotic spacecraft operations during tumbling target capture. Motivated by latest ESA guidelines for safe close proximity operations, safety zones and requirements are translated into dedicated CBFs. The 13-DoF system is decomposed into translational, attitude, and robotic subsystems, each equipped with a safety filter that minimally modifies nominal control inputs in a lightweight quadratic program. The filters enforce a conical approach corridor, collision avoidance zone, attitude line-of-sight pointing, angular velocity limits, robotic joint limits, link-base collision avoidance, and actuator constraints. Dynamic coupling between subsystems is handled by treating upstream safe control commands as known interconnection inputs in the downstream safety filters, preserving modularity while supporting system-level safety. The framework is validated in an on-orbit servicing scenario, including final approach, angular rate synchronization, and tumbling target grasping, using the high-fidelity astrodynamics simulator Basilisk. Monte Carlo simulation results demonstrate runtime efficiency and operational safety for various tumbling rates.

</details>


### [51] [You Should Be Properly Scoring Your Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25900)
*Ola Rønning,Usama Saqib,Andrzej Wąsowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估里程计时不应只用RMSE等点指标，而应使用严格适当评分规则同时评估估计与协方差；作者提出无需真值的单边成对检验，并在smfeval中实现，用于诊断LiDAR-惯性里程计的过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点指标忽略估计器报告的协方差，无法反映估计器是否信任自身输出，也无法按各方向不确定性加权误差，导致在不确定方向上的高误差被过度惩罚。

Method: 采用严格适当评分规则，将估计值与报告的不确定性一起评分；当无协方差时退化为点指标，有协方差时可诊断协方差不一致性。提出单边成对检验，无需真值即可暴露两个估计器中至少一个的过度自信。实现于开源框架smfeval，并以地面LiDAR-惯性里程计平移分量为案例，评估四个滤波器的协方差质量。

Result: 成对检验表明，无需真值即可暴露两个估计器中至少一个的过度自信。在四个LiDAR-惯性里程计滤波器中均发现过度自信，最差情况报告厘米级确定性但误差达千米级。进一步追溯机制，过度自信源于滤波器高估LiDAR测量所携带的新信息量。

Conclusion: 里程计评估应使用严格适当评分规则及单边成对检验，以同时评估估计精度与不确定性质量；smfeval提供了相应工具，并能帮助定位协方差过度自信的成因。

Abstract: When we evaluate the performance of our odometry, it is common practice to score the estimated track against a ground truth. Unfortunately, scoring uses point metrics, such as the root mean square error, that ignore the covariance matrix which estimators like filters and smoothers already report. Using the covariance matters for two reasons. First, the covariance encodes the estimator's uncertainty, so it tells us whether the estimator trusts its own output. An overconfident estimator will not report itself lost. Second, the covariance weights the error in each direction of the estimate. Without the covariance, an estimator is unduly penalized for a high error in an uncertain direction. Instead of point metrics, we should use strictly proper scoring rules. These rules score the estimate together with its reported uncertainty. Strictly proper scoring rules recover the point metrics when no covariance is reported, and they diagnose covariance inconsistency when covariance is reported. Using a one-sided pairwise test, we show that two estimators can expose overconfidence in at least one of them without a ground truth. Strictly proper scoring rules and our pairwise test are available in our open-source framework smfeval. As a case study, we use smfeval to assess the uncertainty quality of the translational component of ground-based LiDAR-inertial odometry. Across four filters we find overconfidence - the worst case reports centimeter certainty with kilometer error. Knowing the filters are overconfident, we investigate the mechanism. The investigation traces overconfidence to filters crediting LiDAR measurements with more new information than they carry.

</details>


### [52] [Robust Active-Perception Control for Global-State-Free Aerial-Ground Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25898)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Jiajun Yu,Baozhe Zhang,Pengxiang Zhou,Wentao Liu,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COPA：一种面向无全局状态空中-地面协同的鲁棒主动感知框架，通过单轴云台解耦相机光轴与无人机俯仰，结合TCN运动预测与MPC联合优化无人机和云台控制，在仿真和实机中实现连续目标可见与鲁棒跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中-地面协同需要实时UAV-UGV相对状态信息。与其为双方维护全局估计，在UGV附着的非惯性系中直接控制可避免依赖全局定位；基于视觉与被动标记的相对位姿估计是低成本有效方案。但固定相机会在所需UAV姿态与视场约束冲突时丢失运动UGV。

Method: 使用单轴云台将相机光轴与UAV俯仰姿态解耦；推导关联UAV运动、云台角度和UGV运动与目标图像平面状态的主动感知模型；采用TCN从近期运动历史预测短时域UGV加速度与角速度，且不依赖全局状态测量；MPC利用这些预测联合优化UAV与云台控制。

Result: 仿真表明COPA能保持连续目标可见性；消融研究证实TCN可降低UGV运动切换期间的峰值误差；真实实验中，在UGV加速度高达3 m/s²、偏航角速度高达1.0 rad/s条件下实现鲁棒跟踪。

Conclusion: COPA是一种无需全局状态、可应对视场约束的主动感知框架，通过云台主动调节与运动预测控制，有效提升空中-地面协同中相对位姿视觉跟踪的连续性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Aerial-ground cooperation requires real-time UAV--UGV relative-state information. Instead of maintaining global estimates for both robots, direct control in a UGV-attached non-inertial frame avoids reliance on global localization. Vision-based relative pose estimation with a passive marker offers a low-cost and effective solution. However, a fixed camera may lose sight of the moving UGV when the required UAV attitude conflicts with the field-of-view (FOV) constraint. To address this, we propose COPA, a robust active-perception framework for global-state-free aerial-ground cooperation. We use a single-axis gimbal to decouple the camera optical axis from the UAV pitch attitude. We derive an active-perception model that relates UAV motion, gimbal angle, and UGV motion to the target image-plane state.A Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) predicts short-horizon UGV acceleration and angular velocity from recent motion history without global-state measurements. The model predictive control (MPC) uses these predictions to jointly optimize UAV and gimbal control. Simulations show that COPA maintains continuous target visibility, while ablation studies confirm that the TCN reduces peak errors during UGV motion transitions. Real-world experiments with UGV accelerations up to 3m/s^2 and yaw rates up to 1.0rad/s demonstrate robust tracking.

</details>


### [53] [What is the Better Curriculum: Controller-Shaped Grasping Behavior for Contact Force-Sensitive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25887)
*Ziyan Feng,Zizhao Yuan,Yulong Fu,Yuxin He,Zhiyuan Zhang,Zhengjie Zhang,Jinni Zhou,Renjing Xu,Qiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将 25 Hz 触觉反射控制器作为数据收集阶段的教师，生成“反射塑形”演示，用于无触觉策略学习，以解决力敏感抓取中人工控制难以维持窄力域的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉策略学习多把触觉作为策略输入；但在亚牛顿级易损物体抓取中，瓶颈更早出现在数据收集：人工控制夹爪延迟大、粒度粗，难以稳定维持抓取所需窄接触力范围。

Method: 提出收集时触觉反射教师：用确定性 25 Hz 触觉反射控制器控制夹爪并采集演示，再训练无触觉输入策略（ACT、π0.5）；推理时可加部署时反射仲裁器用于抗扰。

Result: ACT 在标称塑料杯任务上恢复教师抓取力 profile，稳定抓取率达 95%，显著优于视觉筛选的人工演示；对 π0.5 改善同分布稳定性，并在未见纸杯变体上呈有利探索趋势。随机外部扰动下，仅策略的反射数据 π0.5 仍有 45% 试验失败，而部署时反射仲裁器保持全部抓取。

Conclusion: 触觉在力敏感操作中可扮演收集期教师角色：不将触觉并入策略，而是用其塑形演示行为；但扰动抑制仍依赖控制器，揭示了无触觉策略的边界。

Abstract: How should a robot learn to manipulate objects so fragile that sub-Newton contact forces can cause irreversible damage? Existing visuo-tactile policy learning typically treats tactile sensing as an additional policy input. In direct-contact force-sensitive manipulation, however, the bottleneck can arise earlier, during data collection: manual gripper control is too delayed and coarse-grained to reliably maintain the narrow force range required for stable grasping. We therefore use a deterministic 25 Hz tactile reflex controller as a collection-time teacher, producing demonstrations with controller-shaped grasping behavior for tactile-free policy learning. On Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), policies trained from reflex-shaped demonstrations recover the teacher's grasping profile and achieve 95% stable grasps on the nominal plastic-cup task, substantially outperforming visually screened manual demonstrations. The same intervention improves in-distribution stability on $\pi_{0.5}$ and shows a favorable exploratory trend on an unseen paper-cup variant. Under randomized external disturbance, however, the reflex-data $\pi_{0.5}$ policy still fails in 45% of policy-only trials, whereas a deployment-time reflex arbiter retains all grasps. These results reveal a new role for tactile feedback in force-sensitive manipulation: rather than integrating tactile into the policy, we use it as a collection-time teacher that shapes grasping behavior in demonstrations for policy learning, while disturbance rejection remains controller-dependent, revealing the boundary of tactile-free policy.

</details>


### [54] [Sometimes You Gotta Run Before You Can Walk: Run-then-Walk Scheduling Strategy for VLM Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25831)
*Yuqi Ye,Shangkun Sun,Junhong Lin,Jiayi Zhao,Changhao Peng,Wei Zheng,Guoqing Liu,Tiesong Zhao,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Run-then-Walk 两阶段奖励调度策略，用于 VLM 自动驾驶规划器的 GRPO 强化学习，将“探索高通行效率”与“修复安全性”显式分离，从而兼顾性能与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GRPO 式自动驾驶规划器要么只优化通行效率，带来追求前进但不安全的风险；要么过早施加安全约束，导致行为过度保守，且均需较长训练。作者希望同时解决安全、效率与收敛速度问题。

Method: 先揭示两种不同 RL 阶段/模式：Run-GRPO 对应激进的通行效率探索，Walk-GRPO 对应稳定通行下的安全恢复。据此提出 Run-then-Walk 两阶段奖励调度：Run 阶段专注通行效率，使策略摆脱保守偏置并发现高通行模式；Walk 阶段引入终点与安全策略，修复 Run 阶段的不安全行为。

Result: 在 NAVSIMv1、NAVSIMv2、Navhard 和 nuScenes 等基准上，使用多种 VLM 规划器验证，驾驶性能提升，同时 RL 训练轮数比基线减少 40--50%。

Conclusion: Run-then-Walk 通过反向的两阶段调度，显式分离通行探索与安全修复，克服 Walk-first 的保守性和联合优化的不安全效率追求，在更短训练下实现更优驾驶表现。

Abstract: Recent VLM-based autonomous driving planners adopt GRPO-style reinforcement learning to optimize driving performance. However, existing GRPO recipes either optimize driving efficiency, risking progress-seeking but unsafe behavior, or enforce early safety constraints, leading to overly conservative behavior; both require lengthy training. To solve these problems, we first reveal two distinct RL regimes: a progress regime (Run-GRPO) that aggressively explores high progress, and a safety regime (Walk-GRPO) that restores safety under stable progress. Based on this finding, we propose $\textit{Run-then-Walk}$, a simple yet effective two-stage reward scheduling strategy for GRPO, achieving both better performance and faster convergence. Unlike one-stage RL, which may focus on progress, safety, or a mixture of both within a single training phase, this schedule explicitly separates progress discovery from safety repair. In the $\textit{Run}$ phase, we focus on progress, allowing the policy to escape the conservative bias and discover high-progress modes. In the subsequent $\textit{Walk}$ phase, we introduce endpoint and safety strategy to repair unsafe behaviors from the Run phase. This reversed schedule overcomes the conservatism of Walk-first methods and the unsafe progress-seeking of joint optimization. We validate it with various VLM-based planners on multiple benchmarks: NAVSIMv1, NAVSIMv2, Navhard, and nuScenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved driving performance while requiring 40--50\% fewer RL training epochs than the baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Reconstruction Error: Analytical and Data-Driven Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25820)
*Yuxin Yang,Gaohan He,Changxue Guan,Hangming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文在统一离散动作分词接口下比较固定解析、数据驱动线性和非线性神经动作表征，发现重建保真度与闭环控制性能排名不一致，单靠重建误差无法可靠选择动作表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散动作分词是自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的核心，但动作表征通常主要按重建保真度评估。作者希望识别真正影响闭环控制的表征属性，并检验重建误差能否作为动作表征选择依据。

Method: 在统一tokenization接口下比较固定解析表征、数据驱动线性表征（PCA）和非线性神经表征（autoencoder），并结合率失真分析、序列建模诊断和3,500次LIBERO rollout；通过多策略训练种子与seed-42匹配消融，评估几何保真度、token序列可预测性、解码器稳定性及闭环成功率。

Result: 表征排名随评价标准变化。PCA的名义重建误差低于Temporal-DCT，但产生更不可预测的token序列，在三个策略训练种子中seen-task平均成功率低3.0个百分点，且在一个种子中策略排序反转。seed-42匹配消融中，autoencoder进一步降低重建误差，却未得到最强策略，并对离散token扰动更敏感。

Conclusion: 重建保真度不能单独可靠地选择自回归控制中的动作表征；应联合评估几何保真度、序列可预测性、解码器稳定性和闭环性能。

Abstract: Discrete action tokenization is central to autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, yet action representations are often evaluated primarily through reconstruction fidelity. We ask which representation properties actually matter for closed-loop control by comparing fixed analytical, data-driven linear, and nonlinear neural representations under a unified tokenization interface. Across rate-distortion analysis, sequence-modeling diagnostics, and 3,500 LIBERO rollouts, representation rankings change with the evaluation criterion. PCA achieves lower nominal reconstruction error than Temporal-DCT, but produces less predictable token sequences and 3.0 percentage points lower mean seen-task success across three policy-training seeds, with the policy ordering reversing in one seed. In a matched seed-42 ablation, an autoencoder further reduces reconstruction error yet does not yield the strongest policy and exhibits greater sensitivity to discrete token perturbations. These findings show that reconstruction fidelity alone cannot reliably select action representations for autoregressive control, motivating joint evaluation of geometric fidelity, sequence predictability, decoder stability, and closed-loop performance.

</details>


### [56] [MOLA LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (MOLA-LIO) on the COMFORT Localization Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25813)
*Jose Luis Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本报告介绍团队参加 IROS 2026 COMFORT 定位基准的参赛方案，在 Boxi 载荷采集的 GrandTour 数据集上评估；将 MOLA-LO 扩展为可融合 IMU 与可选腿部运动学里程计的 LiDAR-惯性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在 COMFORT Localization Benchmark 中提升定位表现，并利用 IMU 及腿部运动学信息增强 LiDAR 里程计的鲁棒性与精度。

Method: 将 MOLA-LO 扩展为 LiDAR-惯性系统，除 LiDAR 外还接收 IMU 数据，并可选地融合腿部运动学里程计；文中描述了系统架构、所消费的数据流，以及用于选择提交配置的本地协议。

Result: 在采用 Boxi 载荷记录的 GrandTour 数据集上完成评估，并给出了支持实时性声明的测量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作记录了 COMFORT 基准的参赛方案，验证了融合 IMU 与可选腿部运动学里程计的 MOLA-LO 扩展在 GrandTour/Boxi 数据上的实时定位能力。

Abstract: This short report documents our entry to the COMFORT Localization Benchmark (IROS 2026), evaluated on the GrandTour dataset recorded with the Boxi payload. It extends MOLA-LO into a LiDAR-inertial system that also ingests IMU and, optionally, legged kinematic odometry. We describe the architecture, the streams consumed, the local protocol that selected the submitted configuration, and the measurements backing our real-time claim.

</details>


### [57] [VisForce: Visual Grounding of Current and Desired Forces for Goal-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25785)
*Jung-Woo Lee,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VisForce，将当前与期望接触力以视觉线索形式对齐到指尖位置，并借助目标条件交叉注意力生成力感知动作，用于基于 VLA 的灵巧手操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型已成为通用机器人操作策略，但在灵巧手操作中，接触力通常以独立状态或力专用表示提供，难以显式建模力与其对应视觉位置之间的空间对应关系。

Method: VisForce 将当前力和期望力渲染为对应指尖位置上的视觉力线索，分别叠加到当前手腕图像和任务特定目标图像上，再通过目标条件交叉注意力融合两种表示，从而生成力感知动作。

Result: 在配备 RH56F1 灵巧手的真实 UR10 机器人上，通过力条件抓取和三个多阶段操作任务评估。力条件抓取中，握力随期望力增加表现出一致响应；鸡蛋和牙膏管的抓取-提起成功率分别为 70% 和 80%。杯子插入/倒瓶、夹子辅助面包转移、滑动调制轴孔装配的最终成功率分别为 70%、55% 和 40%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，指尖对齐的视觉力表示可有效用于基于 VLA 的灵巧手操作中的力感知条件控制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as general-purpose robotic manipulation policies. However, in dexterous hand manipulation, contact forces are typically provided as separate states or force-specific representations, making it difficult to explicitly represent the spatial correspondence between force and their corresponding visual locations. In this work, we propose VisForce, which visually grounds the current and desired forces at their corresponding fingertip locations. VisForce renders current and desired visual force cues on the current wrist image and a task-specific goal image, and combines the two representations through goal-conditioned cross-attention to generate force-aware actions. We evaluate VisForce using a real UR10 robot equipped with an RH56F1 dexterous hand through force-conditioned grasping and three multi-stage manipulation tasks. In force-conditioned grasping experiments, VisForce exhibited a consistent grip-force response as the desired force increased, and achieved grasp-and-lift success rates of 70% and 80% for an egg and a toothpaste tube, respectively. It further achieved final success rates of 70%, 55%, and 40% on cup insertion/bottle pouring, tong-assisted bread transfer, and slip-modulated peg-in-hole, respectively. These results show that fingertip-aligned visual force representations can be effectively used for force-aware conditioning in VLA-based dexterous hand manipulation.

</details>


### [58] [MedVLA: A Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Framework for Closed-Loop Precision Medical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25756)
*Junjie Xie,Chuxuan He,Angen Ye,Yujia Song,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MedVLA，一个将高层多模态推理与底层函数约束执行相结合的分层框架，用于精准医疗机器人，并在柔性电极植入闭环试验中以95.0%成功率显著优于OpenVLA和π0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准医疗机器人需在严格安全性、可解释性和执行约束下进行自适应决策；现有VLA模型的连续动作生成范式不适合该类任务，且可靠闭环操作可能依赖非动作的系统功能调用。

Method: MedVLA采用分层框架：高层进行多模态推理，低层执行受函数约束的动作；并引入可扩展多智能体流水线生成技能导向的思维链（CoT）数据，以进行结构化训练；可基于不同多模态大模型骨干进行微调。

Result: 在相同初始条件下进行100次闭环柔性电极植入试验，MedVLA任务成功率达95.0%，在准确性、稳定性和安全性上显著优于代表性VLA基线，如OpenVLA（8%）和π0（15%）。

Conclusion: 结构化推理结合受约束的函数级执行，是通向可部署精准医疗机器人的可行路径。

Abstract: Precision medical robotics demands adaptive decision-making under strict safety, interpretability, and execution constraints. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show strong multimodal reasoning ability, their continuous action generation paradigm is not well suited for precision medical tasks, where reliable closed-loop operation may also depend on non-action system function calls. To address this gap, we propose MedVLA, a hierarchical framework that couples high-level multimodal reasoning with low-level function-constrained execution. We further introduce a scalable multi-agent pipeline to generate skill-oriented chain-of-thought(CoT) data for structured training. Built on different multimodal large-model backbones, MedVLA consistently improves performance after fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework across model variants. Under identical initial conditions, we perform 100 closed-loop flexible electrode implantation trials. The results show that MedVLA achieves a 95.0\% task success rate, substantially outperforming representative VLA baselines, including OpenVLA (8\%) and $\pi_0$ (15\%), in accuracy, stability, and safety. These results indicate that structured reasoning with constrained function-level execution is a practical route toward deployable precision medical robotics.

</details>


### [59] [PLAT: Sparse Timed Keyframe Motion Tracking for Humanoid Control via Privileged Latent Transition Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25754)
*Zepeng Wang,Jiangxing Wang,Chao Ma,Xiaochuan Shi,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PLAT，一种三阶段稀疏定时关键帧运动跟踪策略学习框架，利用特权潜在转移学习，使策略在部署时仅接收稀疏未来关键帧及其期望到达时间，即可执行稳定全身运动并连续到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形运动跟踪策略依赖逐帧稠密参考，限制了其作为高层运动控制器用于规划与交互式运动生成；论文研究稀疏定时关键帧运动跟踪，要求策略仅凭稀疏关键帧和到达时间实现稳定全身运动控制。

Method: PLAT 包含三阶段：首先预训练稠密跟踪专家以提供鲁棒运动先验；其次通过 DAgger 式模仿学习特权潜在先验；最后进行潜在残差强化学习，优化潜在转移而非直接优化动作。训练时利用稠密目标序列作为特权监督，部署时仅需稀疏定时关键帧命令。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，PLAT 在不同规划时域下均能保持准确稳定的稀疏定时关键帧跟踪，尤其在长时域命令下表现突出；并成功部署于 Unitree G1 人形机器人，验证了其有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: PLAT 有效桥接了稠密运动跟踪与稀疏目标条件控制，证明了其在稀疏人形运动控制中的有效性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking policies rely on dense frame-by-frame references, limiting their use as high-level motion controllers for planning and interactive motion generation. We study \emph{Sparse Timed Keyframe Motion Tracking}, where a policy receives only sparse future keyframes and their desired arrival times, and must execute stable whole-body motions that reach successive goals. We propose \textbf{PLAT}, a three-stage sparse timed keyframe motion tracking policy learning framework with \textbf{P}rivileged \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{T}ransition learning. PLAT bridges dense motion tracking and sparse goal-conditioned control by exploiting dense goal sequences as privileged supervision during training while requiring only sparse timed keyframe commands at deployment. A pretrained dense tracking expert first provides robust motion priors. A privileged latent prior is then learned through DAgger-style imitation, followed by latent residual reinforcement learning that refines latent transitions instead of directly optimizing actions. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that PLAT maintains accurate and stable sparse timed keyframe tracking across varying planning horizons, with particularly strong performance under long-horizon commands. Successful deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot further demonstrates the effectiveness and practicality of PLAT for sparse humanoid motion control.

</details>


### [60] [Fisheye-VLA: Decoupling Coverage and Acuity for Manipulation with a Single Fisheye Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25750)
*Ziang Ren,Zike Yan,Raymond Zhang,Xuguo He,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fisheye-VLA 提出仅用单个被动鱼眼相机同时提供全局场景感知与局部细节反馈，通过局部透视裁剪和端到端 VLA 集成，在无需腕部相机的情况下完成多种操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机配置需要分别用前视相机提供全局场景感知、用腕部相机提供局部精细反馈；本文希望用单一鱼眼相机统一这两类视觉能力。

Method: 该方法使用单个被动鱼眼：全局视图保留工作空间，局部透视裁剪将视觉预算导向交互区域。作者通过受控重渲染研究，在相同录制观测上比较不同裁剪方向，发现以末端执行器为中心的视图能获得更大候选池中的大部分估计收益，因此围绕双手进行紧凑分配。接口利用标定的末端执行器投影和运动前瞻来跟踪裁剪，并用共享射线编码在裁剪移动时保持其空间含义，最终与预训练 VLA 集成。

Result: 在两个扩展桌面区域中分别达到 84% 和 82% 的成功率，其中部分目标放置超出前置相机覆盖范围，并支持货架和传送带操作。消融实验表明，在更大的工作空间区域中，局部裁剪及其观察方向变得更加重要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单个鱼眼相机即可支持这些操作任务，无需物理腕部相机。

Abstract: Manipulation requires both broad scene awareness and detailed local feedback, yet conventional camera rigs provide them through separate front and wrist cameras. We present Fisheye-VLA, a visual interface that brings these capabilities together using a single passive fisheye. A global view preserves the workspace, while local perspective crops direct detail toward the interaction. The key design question is where this local visual budget should go. We answer it through a controlled re-rendering study, comparing alternative crop directions on the same recorded observations. The study finds that end-effector-centered views capture most of the estimated benefit of a much larger candidate pool, motivating a compact allocation around both hands. Our interface uses calibrated end-effector projection and motion lead to track the crops, while a shared ray encoding preserves their spatial meaning as they move. Integrated with a pretrained VLA, it achieves 84% and 82% success in the two expanded tabletop regions, where some target placements extend beyond the front-camera coverage, and supports shelf and conveyor manipulation. Ablations show that local crops and their viewing directions become more important in the larger workspace regions. The results demonstrate that a single fisheye can support these manipulation tasks without physical wrist cameras.

</details>


### [61] [Dual Covariance Gaussian Splatting SLAM: Decoupling Rendering and Registration for Robust Real-Time Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25746)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对基于ICP的3D高斯泼溅SLAM中单个高斯协方差同时服务于渲染与配准所导致的冲突，提出双协方差参数化：每个高斯保留单一均值但拥有渲染协方差与跟踪协方差。方法在TUM RGB-D、ScanNet、Replica及两个RealSense D435i室外序列上实现鲁棒跟踪、降低里程计漂移，并以约60 FPS运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ICP-based 3DGS SLAM中，传入帧通过配准到地图高斯进行实时跟踪，而每个高斯的协方差同时用于渲染和配准。这两种用途对协方差需求相互冲突：建图器优化协方差以最小化光度误差，常将其压扁至表面；而鲁棒配准通常更受益于测量不确定性。因此，单一协方差难以同时满足渲染与跟踪需求。

Method: 提出双协方差参数化：每个高斯保持一个均值，但维护两个协方差。其一是由建图器优化的渲染协方差；其二是由RGB-D传感器噪声模型导出的跟踪协方差。进一步将跟踪协方差用作图像角点的“高斯锚点”，在深度几何较弱的方向上提供约束。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D、ScanNet、Replica以及两个使用RealSense D435i采集的室外序列（轮式与手持平台）上进行评估。方法在多种场景中实现鲁棒跟踪，降低里程计漂移，同时跟踪速度约为60 FPS。

Conclusion: 双协方差参数化有效解耦了3DGS SLAM中渲染与配准对协方差的不同需求，并借助角点高斯锚点增强弱深度几何方向约束，从而在保持实时性的同时提升跟踪鲁棒性并减少漂移。

Abstract: ICP-based 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM tracks in real time by registering incoming frames against map Gaussians, using each primitive's covariance for both rendering and registration. These two uses place conflicting demands on one covariance. The mapper shapes it to minimize photometric error, often flattening it against surfaces, while robust registration typically benefits from measurement uncertainty. We propose a dual-covariance parameterization. Each Gaussian keeps a single mean but holds two covariances: a rendering covariance optimized by the mapper, and a tracking covariance derived from an RGB-D sensor noise model. We further use the tracking covariances as Gaussian anchors for image corners, providing constraints in directions where depth geometry is weak. We evaluate on TUM RGB-D, ScanNet, Replica, and two outdoor sequences recorded with a RealSense D435i on wheeled and handheld platforms. We achieve robust tracking performance across multiple scenes and reduced odometry drift, while tracking at $\sim$ 60 FPS.

</details>


### [62] [AgriGen: Large-Scale Scene Generation Framework for Photorealistic Agricultural Robotics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25725)
*Utkarsh Bajpai,Serge Tleiji,Cédric Pradalier,Stéphanie Aravecchia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于 Isaac Sim 并集成 ROS 的农业环境大规模程序化生成框架，用于缓解农业机器人研究中数据获取难、评估受限和系统鲁棒性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人发展受限于田间准入困难、田间条件不可控、地理差异和作物季节性周期，导致多样农业数据集获取困难且成本高，进而造成评估不足和系统鲁棒性下降；其他机器人领域已通过高保真仿真实现规模化学习与评估，而农业机器人仍缺乏同等能力的工具。

Method: 构建一个 ROS 集成的仿真框架，基于 Isaac Sim，支持农业环境的大规模程序化生成、照片级真实感渲染、物理仿真和领域随机化，并内置对行栽作物、果园和葡萄园的支持，且可扩展至其他作物类别。

Result: 该框架能够在与机器人研究相关的规模上生成农业环境，支持照片级真实感渲染、物理仿真和领域随机化；内置行栽作物、果园和葡萄园场景，并具备面向新增作物类别的直接扩展能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为农业机器人提供了可扩展的高保真仿真与评估工具，有望改善农业数据集稀缺、评估不足和系统鲁棒性受限的问题，并推动农业机器人学习与评测的规模化发展。

Abstract: Agricultural robotics is advancing rapidly, yet progress remains constrained by limited field access, lack of control over field conditions, geographic variability, and seasonal crop cycles. These factors make it difficult and costly to acquire diverse agricultural datasets, resulting in limited evaluation and reduced system robustness. While other robotics domains have scaled learning and evaluation through high-fidelity simulation, agricultural robotics still lacks comparably capable tools. In this paper, we present a ROS-integrated framework, built on Isaac Sim, for large-scale procedural generation of agricultural environments. The framework supports photorealistic rendering, physics simulation, and domain randomization at scales relevant to robotics research, with built-in support for row crops, orchards, and vineyards and straightforward extensibility to additional crop categories. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [63] [Designing an Efficient Excavator Bucket for Lunar ISRU: A Comparative Study with Vision-Based Fill and Displacement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25724)
*Abdulla Hil Kafi,Tomoki Koshi,Jorge Casir,Kenji Nagaoka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出螺旋腔轮式月壤挖掘装置及轻量传感评估栈，可联合估计填充率、沉陷与比能；台架实验中挖掘速率达基线2.2–3.0倍，比能较桶鼓基线降低29%，沉陷更低，DEM可复现趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向月壤挖掘与资源利用任务，在质量、结构和能效约束下，需要比传统重型多执行器挖掘机更简单高效的方案，并需轻量传感手段在线评估挖掘性能。

Method: 设计螺旋腔轮挖掘机构；构建传感器-光照评估栈，基于视觉估计填充率与沉陷，基于执行器日志计算比能；在台架以5、10、15 RPM四转测试，与两种文献基线对比；提出与填充率相关的保持指数η，并建立DEM仿真复现实验趋势。

Result: 相比两种基线，螺旋腔轮获得更高挖掘质量和填充率；挖掘速率提高2.2–3.0倍，比能较桶鼓基线降低29%；归一化沉陷(mm/kg)更低，牵引稳定不陷滞；力矩-时间曲线包络平稳，切割-携带-卸载循环可重复；DEM与实验趋势吻合且误差低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当任务以质量、简洁性和能效为驱动时，螺旋腔轮可替代更重的多执行器挖掘机。

Abstract: This paper present a spiral-cavity wheel for lunar regolith excavation and a sensor-light evaluation stack that jointly estimates fill ratio (vision), sinkage (vision), and specific energy from actuator logs. In benchtop tests (four revolutions at 5, 10, and 15~RPM) against two literature baselines, the proposed wheel achieved higher excavated mass and fill ratio, delivering 2.2-3.0 times higher excavation rate while reducing specific energy by 29 % relative to a bucket-drum baseline. Normalized sinkage (mm/kg) was also lower, indicating stable traction without bogging. Effort-time traces show a steady torque envelope with repeatable cut-carry-dump cycles across speeds. We provide a retention index $\eta$ that correlates with fill ratio and a DEM setup that reproduces experimental trends with low error. Results suggest spiral-cavity wheels can replace heavier multi-actuator diggers when mass, simplicity, and energy efficiency are mission drivers.

</details>


### [64] [Zephyron: Integrated Design and Analytical Evaluation of a Solar-Assisted Mobile Manipulator for Multimodal Environmental Reconnaissance and Distributed Visual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25709)
*Sabik Bin Sultan,Shafi Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Zephyron 四轮漫游车的文献知情工程设计：前置机械臂、环境传感器、分布式计算机视觉、本地记录和后置太阳能模块，用显式组件与几何基线替代缺乏依据的数值假设，形成可追溯的设计与评估框架，但尚需实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境侦察需要能携带传感器、保留测量上下文，并在能量与通信受限下返回可解释证据的移动平台；现有原型中的数值假设缺乏充分支撑。

Method: 进行可复现文献检索，获得 5000 条记录（4858 条唯一）并筛选，结合原始文献与厂商文档定向综述；建立基线：165 mm 轮、12 kg 质量预算、72 Wh 电池能量、20 W 光伏，滚动阻力系数 0.04。开展坡度牵引、能量续航、传感器建模、质量感知停采策略、轻量检测器、参考式传感器学习和数据完整性检查等分析与机器学习评估路径，并提供可编辑 3D 模型、子系统图和可复现分析数据。

Result: 在等负载分配下，10 度稳态爬坡每轮约需 0.517 N·m；40 W 运动负载下，57.6 Wh 可用能量对应约 1.44 h，25% 行驶占空比且无太阳能输入时约 4.19 h；这些均为计算场景而非实测性能。传感器模型表明积分时间、校准、温度和通信延迟约束解释，质量感知停采策略将约束连接到任务执行。

Conclusion: 贡献是带可编辑 3D 模型、子系统图和可复现分析数据的可追溯设计与评估框架；在赋予有效载荷、续航、检测或野外运行等级前，必须进行实验验证。

Abstract: Environmental reconnaissance needs mobile platforms that carry sensors, preserve measurement context, and return interpretable evidence under limited energy and communication. We present a literature-informed engineering design for Zephyron, a four-wheel rover with a front manipulator, environmental sensors, distributed computer vision, local recording, and a raised rear solar module. The design keeps the prototype layout but replaces unsupported numerical assumptions with an explicit component and geometry baseline. A reproducible search retrieved 5,000 records (4,858 unique) for screening, followed by targeted review of primary literature and manufacturer documentation. The baseline uses 165 mm wheels, a 12 kg mass budget, a 72 Wh battery-energy basis, and a 20 W photovoltaic module. With rolling-resistance coefficient 0.04, steady ascent of a 10 degree grade needs about 0.517 N m per wheel under equal load sharing. An illustrative 40 W motion load gives 1.44 h from 57.6 Wh usable energy, and a 25 percent driving duty gives 4.19 h without solar input; these are calculated scenarios, not measured performance. Sensor models show how integration time, calibration, temperature, and communication delay constrain interpretation, and a quality-aware stop-and-sample policy links these constraints to mission execution. Lightweight detectors, reference-based sensor learning, and executable data-integrity checks define a reproducible machine-learning evaluation pathway. The contribution is a traceable design and evaluation framework with editable 3D models, subsystem diagrams, and reproducible analytical data. Experimental validation is required before assigning payload, endurance, detection, or field-operating ratings.

</details>


### [65] [The Cartesian Hand: In-Hand Manipulation with All-Linear Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25696)
*Boxi Xia,Bokuan Li,Ryan Shin,Zijiang Yang,Jiaxun Liu,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种7自由度末端执行器Cartesian Hand，用两个独立平行夹爪与四个平动指尖，仅通过线性运动实现抓取与相对操作；在35个物体上完成开合瓶盖、移液、泵送、双手柄操作、拧螺丝、触发器和抓取内重定向，并可迁移至人形机器人双臂操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手虽自由度高但机械与控制复杂，平行夹爪简单鲁棒却难以在抓取后操作物体；操作螺纹容器、制造工具和实验仪器等铰接物体常需第二夹爪、外部夹具或协调臂运动。

Method: 设计7自由度Cartesian Hand，将独立抓取与相对操作集成于单一末端执行器，仅依赖线性运动。两个独立驱动的平行夹爪分别夹持物体不同部分，四个可平移指尖产生相对运动；其与构型无关的指尖运动学使操作可由简单线性运动基元组合而成，尤其适合螺纹、枢轴、线性导轨、柱塞和扳机等机构。

Result: 演示了开合瓶盖、移液、泵送、双手柄操作、拧螺丝、扳机驱动和抓取内重定向，覆盖实验室、制造和家庭场景的35个物体；相同操作流程可从固定基座机械臂迁移至人形机器人，并用两个Cartesian Hand完成双臂实验室操作。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当独立抓取与相对运动被直接设计进末端执行器时，机械简单架构也能产生通用的手内操作能力；作者将开源全部软硬件设计。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has increasingly pursued human-like dexterous hands with many articulated degrees of freedom, offering rich manipulation capabilities at the cost of mechanical and control complexity. At the other extreme, parallel grippers are simple and robust, but provide little ability to manipulate an object after grasping it. Operating articulated objects such as threaded containers, manufacturing tools, and laboratory instruments often requires a second gripper, an external fixture, or coordinated arm motion. We introduce the Cartesian Hand, a 7-DoF end-effector that rethinks dexterous manipulation by combining independent grasping and relative manipulation within a single end-effector using only linear motion. Two independently actuated parallel grippers hold different parts of an object, while four translating fingertips generate relative motion between the grasped parts. Its configuration-independent fingertip kinematics allow manipulation to be composed from simple linear motion primitives. The Cartesian Hand is particularly suited to objects structured around common mechanisms such as threads, pivots, linear guides, plungers, and triggers. We demonstrate cap opening and closing, pipetting, pumping, two-handle manipulation, screwdriving, trigger actuation, and in-grasp reorientation across 35 objects spanning laboratory, manufacturing, and household settings. The same manipulation procedures transfer from a fixed-base robot arm to a humanoid, where we demonstrate bimanual laboratory manipulation using two Cartesian Hands. These results show that versatile in-hand manipulation capability can emerge from a mechanically simple architecture when independent grasping and relative motion are designed directly into the end-effector. We will open-source all software and hardware design. Our website is this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [Induced Riemannian Metrics for Motion Planning with Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25695)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Thomas Cohn,Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出，在约束运动规划中，路径长度应由子流形从构型空间黎曼度量继承的诱导度量来度量；隐式与显式表示会给出同一诱导度量（仅坐标不同），因此度量选择与表示选择可解耦，并将黎曼度量规划扩展到约束子流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务约束和闭环约束将机器人运动限制在构型空间的低维弯曲子流形上；路径长度依赖度量，欧氏度量处处同价，而一般黎曼度量（如动能度量）随方向和构型变化。现有方法中，隐式约束水平集通常配合构型空间欧氏度量，显式参数化通常配合参数域度量，导致规划器最小化的路径长度依赖表示。

Method: 采用子流形从构型空间黎曼度量继承的诱导度量来定义路径长度；证明隐式表示与显式表示产生同一诱导度量，只是坐标表达不同，且该结论对任意黎曼度量成立。基于此，将黎曼度量下的规划从无约束空间推广到约束子流形，并同时用于采样规划器和轨迹优化器；在显式表示中，诱导度量还计入参数化引入的畸变。

Result: 理论上得到表示无关性：隐式与显式表示对应相同诱导几何。方法上实现了在约束子流形上使用黎曼度量的采样规划与轨迹优化。实验在双臂Franka机械臂、带末端执行器任务约束的双臂操作设置中比较欧氏度量与动能度量；摘要未报告具体数值或性能结论。

Conclusion: 约束运动规划中的度量选择可与表示选择独立；应使用子流形诱导度量，以在隐式或显式表示下保持相同几何，并支持比欧氏度量更一般的黎曼度量，从而统一约束子流形上的规划与优化。

Abstract: In constrained motion planning problems, task and loop-closure constraints restrict a robot's motion to a curved, lower-dimensional submanifold of its configuration space. Planners measure path length with a metric, which sets the cost of moving in each direction. Under the Euclidean metric, this cost is the same everywhere, whereas under a general Riemannian metric, such as the kinetic-energy metric, the cost can vary with direction and configuration. Existing methods often describe the submanifold either implicitly, as a constraint level set, or explicitly, through a parameterization. The implicit representation is typically combined with the Euclidean metric of the configuration space, and the explicit representation with the parameter domain, so the path length that a planner minimizes depends on the representation. Instead, we measure path length with the induced metric, which the submanifold inherits from a Riemannian metric on the configuration space. The implicit and explicit representations yield the same induced metric, expressed in different coordinates, and hence the same geometry. This result holds for any Riemannian metric on the configuration space, not only the Euclidean one. The choice of metric is therefore independent of the choice of representation. Using this result, we extend planning under a Riemannian metric from unconstrained spaces to constraint submanifolds by applying the induced metric in both a sampling-based planner and a trajectory optimizer. For an explicit representation, the induced metric also accounts for the distortion that the parameterization introduces. In experiments on a bimanual manipulation setup with two Franka arms under end-effector task constraints, we compare the Euclidean and kinetic-energy metrics.

</details>


### [67] [MotionForge: A Data Generation Pipeline and Large-Scale Benchmark for Long-Horizon Manipulation of Dynamic Objects with Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25689)
*Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Haotian Xian,Ruyang Han,Jiayi Sun,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Luxi Li,Kaimin Mao,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 MotionForge，这是首个面向动态操作的大规模仿真基准与数据生成流程，用于联合评估域偏移和长时程交互；它包含40个动态交互任务、11种运动模式及17个长时程任务，并引入单因素/联合域偏移评估协议和延迟感知的解耦执行协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的机器人策略主要在静态或准静态环境中评估，而动态操作中物体与场景持续变化；当前动态仿真基准多关注短时程、反应式交互和简单运动模式，且缺乏对域偏移下系统评估和模型无关实时执行协议的支持。

Method: 构建 MotionForge 仿真基准与数据生成流程，涵盖40个动态交互任务、11种运动模式、17个长时程任务；提出两种新协议：(1) 在单因素（如仅背景变化）和联合域偏移（如物体、背景、光照和速度同时变化）下评估策略鲁棒性的系统评估协议；(2) 解耦且延迟感知的执行协议，使环境演化独立于策略推理时间。

Result: 对代表性通用机器人策略的广泛评估表明，策略在联合域偏移下存在显著局限，暴露出当前策略能力与动态物体在域偏移下鲁棒长时程操作需求之间的关键差距。

Conclusion: MotionForge 为具身智能未来研究提供了一个全面的测试平台，推动动态操作中鲁棒长时程策略的评估与发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot policies have demonstrated promising progress, yet they are predom- inantly evaluated in static or quasi-static environments. In dynamic manipulation, objects and scenes continuously evolve while the robot perceives, reasons, and acts. However, recent dynamic simulation benchmarks largely focus on short-horizon, reactive interactions with simple motion patterns and offer limited support for both systematic evaluation under domain shifts and model-agnostic real-time execution protocols. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MotionForge, the first large- scale simulation benchmark and data-generation pipeline tailored to jointly evaluate domain shifts and long-horizon interaction in dynamic manipulation. MotionForge comprises 40 dynamic interaction tasks spanning 11 distinct motion patterns, with dedicated support for 17 long-horizon tasks. Our benchmark introduces two key novelties: (1) a systematic evaluation protocol for assessing policy robustness under both single-factor (e.g., only backgrounds shift) and joint domain shifts (e.g., simultaneous shifts of objects, backgrounds, lighting, and speed); and (2) a decoupled, latency-aware execution protocol where the environ- ment continuously evolves independently of policy inference time. Extensive evaluations of representative general-purpose robot policies on our benchmark reveal substantial limitations under joint domain shifts. These findings expose a critical gap between current policy capabilities and the requirements of robust long- horizon manipulation of dynamic objects under domain shifts, establishing MotionForge as a comprehensive testbed for future research in embodied AI.

</details>


### [68] [MatcherCompass: A Deployment-Aware Benchmark to Guide Image Matcher Selection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25688)
*Hyunwoo Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MatcherCompass 是面向野外机器人部署的局部特征匹配器选择基准，跨硬件、分辨率、数值精度和视觉条件评测九种匹配流程，并给出满足时间与资源限制的选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人需在昼夜、可见光/热成像等条件下，于机载时间与资源预算内获得准确图像对应；单设备单方法的精度与耗时报告不足以指导目标平台上的匹配器及其配置选择。

Method: 在统一输入和位姿评估流程下，比较九种经典与学习型匹配流程，覆盖四种图像分辨率和支持的数值精度；四种视觉条件包括视角变化、可见光昼夜匹配、热成像昼夜匹配以及白天可见光—热成像匹配。使用5°、10°、20°误差—召回曲线下面积（AUC）评估位姿精度，并在四类GPU平台（工作站与机载计算机）上测量每对图像的运行时间、GPU显存和能耗。

Result: 硬件、输入分辨率和数值精度的变化可使匹配器跨越运行时间预算边界，从而改变可行选择。测量结果被组织为选型指南，返回满足用户指定时间与资源限制的所有配置，并给出其在所选视觉条件下的精度。

Conclusion: MatcherCompass 为根据机器人传感条件和计算硬件选择匹配流程提供了实测依据。

Abstract: Field robots operating across time of day and sensing modalities require accurate image correspondences within onboard time and resource budgets. However, accuracy and runtime reported for individual methods on a single device provide limited guidance for choosing a matcher and its configuration on a target platform. We present MatcherCompass, a deployment-aware benchmark for choosing local feature matchers in field robotics. Under common input and pose-evaluation procedures, we compare nine classical and learned matching pipelines across four image resolutions and supported numerical precisions. Four visual conditions cover viewpoint variation, day--night matching in visible and thermal imagery, and daytime visible--thermal matching. We evaluate pose accuracy using the area under the error--recall curve (AUC) at $5^\circ$, $10^\circ$, and $20^\circ$, and measure runtime, GPU memory, and energy per image pair on four GPU platforms spanning workstation and onboard computers. The results show that changes in hardware, input resolution, and numerical precision can move a matcher across a runtime budget boundary, altering the feasible choices. We organize the measurements into a selection guide that returns all configurations satisfying user-specified time and resource limits, together with their accuracy under the selected visual condition. MatcherCompass provides measured evidence for choosing matching pipelines that fit a robot's sensing conditions and computing hardware. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [69] [SG-CPG: Severity-Gated Central Pattern Generators for Adaptive Quadruped Locomotion under Continuous Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25687)
*Adarsh Kumar Kosta,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SG-CPG，一种面向持续执行器退化的四足CPG控制框架；它保留冻结的健康CPG策略，并通过损伤严重度驱动的门控机制维持运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物在肢体无力时通常不直接切换步态，而是卸载受损肢体、重新协调其余肢体，并随损伤严重度缩放反应。受此启发，作者希望将健康运动策略扩展到渐进式执行器退化，而不是把故障处理为离散失效。

Method: SG-CPG保留冻结的健康CPG策略，引入两个严重度驱动门：残差门重新协调四条腿，振幅门随退化加剧逐步缩短无力腿的步幅。通过降低关节力矩上限和缩放低层控制器增益两种机制模拟渐进退化，并在Unitree Go2仿真与真机上验证。

Result: 仿真中，在95%关节强度损失下，SG-CPG于全向指令序列维持trot步态，存活率100%，指令跟踪误差在8%以内。降低力矩上限时，在80%损失下，移除残差严重度观测或振幅门会使无力关节截断步数比例从4.4%分别升至13.6%和26.3%。真机Go2在高达93%小腿力矩退化下，29次前进与转向试验中成功28次。

Conclusion: 严重度门控自适应能够将健康运动策略扩展到渐进式执行器退化，而无需将故障视为离散失效。

Abstract: An animal with a weakened limb does not necessarily switch its gait, instead it unloads the affected limb, re-coordinates the remaining limbs, and scales its response with injury severity. This graded adaptation allows locomotion to persist despite partial loss of limb strength, rather than requiring a discrete transition between healthy and failed. Inspired by this behavior, we propose SG-CPG, a central pattern generator (CPG) for quadruped locomotion under continuous actuator degradation. SG-CPG preserves a frozen healthy CPG policy and introduces two severity-driven gates: a residual gate that re-coordinates all four legs and an amplitude gate that progressively shortens the weakened leg's stride as degradation increases. We emulate progressive degradation through two mechanisms: lowering the joint torque ceiling (ceiling mechanism) and scaling its low-level controller gains (gain mechanism), representing distinct forms of actuator weakening. Our simulations on a Unitree Go2 show that SG-CPG maintains a trot gait with 100% survival across an omnidirectional command schedule under 95% joint strength loss while tracking commands within 8%. Under a lowered torque ceiling, removing either severity path, the residual's severity observation or the amplitude gate, raises clipping at the weakened joint from 4.4% to 13.6% and 26.3% of steps at an 80% loss. On a real Go2, SG-CPG survives 28 of 29 forward and turning trials with up to 93% calf torque degradation. These results show that severity-gated adaptation can extend a healthy locomotion policy to progressive actuator degradation without treating the fault as a discrete failure.

</details>


### [70] [Teaching Reinforcement Learning and Humanoid Robotics to High-School Students: An Expert-Validated Curriculum Design on a Low-Cost Open Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25674)
*Yuanzhe Dong,Jie Cao,Shuman Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向高中生的整合式机器人课程框架，以共享机器人项目串联组装、仿真、强化学习策略训练与真机部署，并通过专家评审明确关键设计张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本开源机器人与强化学习仿真工具为中学生接触前沿机器人学创造了机会，但将机械装配、电气设置、仿真、策略学习、系统辨识和物理部署等完整科研流程转化为适合初学者的连贯课程仍很困难。

Method: 作者设计了一个围绕共享机器人作品组织的课程框架，包含并行学科轨道、基于技术依赖的排序、仿真与硬件的渐进整合、分层性能检查点，以及兼顾团队协作与个人问责的结构；并以高中课程为例，让学生两人一组组装开源人形机器人、在仿真中训练行走策略并部署到实体平台。框架通过迭代设计流程开发，并由机器人研究、工程、中学STEM教育和课程设计领域的五位专家进行形成性评审。

Result: 专家反馈指出三个核心设计张力：真实性与认知负荷、系统集成与及时可见进展、团队建设与个人问责；这些张力被用于完善最终框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为将机器人研究流程转化为跨学科大学前课程提供了结构化方法，未来仍需课堂研究来检验实施效果与学生学习成效。

Abstract: Lower cost open source robots and reinforcement learning (RL) simulation tools create new opportunities for precollege students to engage with contemporary robotics. However, translating a complete research workflow, spanning mechanical assembly, electrical setup, simulation, policy learning, system identification, and physical deployment, into a coherent course for novice learners remains challenging. We present an integrated robotics course framework that organizes these activities around a shared robotic artifact. The framework combines parallel disciplinary tracks, sequencing based on technical dependencies, progressive integration of simulation and hardware, layered performance checkpoints, and structures for balancing collaborative work with individual accountability. We illustrate the framework through a high school curriculum organized around a robot project in which pairs of students assemble an open source humanoid robot, train a walking policy in simulation, and deploy it on the physical platform. The framework was developed through an iterative design process that included formative review by five experts in robotics research, engineering, secondary STEM education, and curriculum design. Expert feedback highlighted three central design tensions: authenticity versus cognitive load, system integration versus timely visible progress, and team construction versus individual accountability. These tensions informed the final framework presented in this paper. This work offers a structured approach for adapting robotics research workflows into interdisciplinary precollege courses; future classroom studies are needed to examine implementation and student learning.

</details>


### [71] [CDKF-Track: Cluster-aware Data-Driven Kalman Filtering for Cooperative 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25668)
*Maria Damanaki,Nikos Piperigkos,Alexandros Gkillas,Aris S. Lalos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CDKFTrack，一种面向协同3D多目标跟踪的聚类感知数据驱动卡尔曼滤波框架，在V2V4Real数据集上相比现有多智能体MOT方法最高提升27.99%的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体MOT在复杂场景中受遮挡、传感器噪声和局部场景理解限制；多智能体系统虽提升鲁棒性，但引入冗余观测，导致错误数据关联，且难以建模非线性目标运动。

Method: CDKFTrack首先通过图拉普拉斯公式融合多车3D LiDAR检测；采用聚类感知冗余削减方案，将空间相关检测分组并选择代表性观测，减少重复输入；随后使用数据驱动卡尔曼滤波从数据中学习目标运动动力学，降低对预设线性运动假设的依赖；并引入基于小波的时间细化模块，利用多分辨率分解抑制短期位置波动、提升轨迹连续性。

Result: 在真实V2V4Real数据集上，CDKFTrack相比最先进的多智能体MOT方法，跟踪精度最高提升27.99%。

Conclusion: CDKFTrack是首个在协同3D MOT中同时解决检测级融合冗余与可学习运动建模的框架，实验验证其有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) is essential for EdgeAI perception systems, where accurate object localization and reliable identification enable safe decision-making. Singleagent MOT suffers from occlusions, sensor noise, and partial scene understanding in complex real-world scenarios. While multi-agent systems improve robustness by exploiting shared information, they introduce redundant measurements that lead to false data associations, and still struggle to capture nonlinear object dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose CDKFTrack, a Cluster-aware Data-Driven Kalman Filtering framework for Cooperative 3D MOT. The proposed method first fuses multivehicle 3D LiDAR detections through a Graph Laplacian-based formulation. Then, a cluster-aware redundancy reduction scheme groups spatially related detections and selects representative observations to reduce duplicate inputs to the tracker. The resulting detections are processed by a data-driven Kalman filter that learns object motion dynamics from data, reducing dependence on predefined linear motion assumptions. Furthermore, a wavelet-based temporal refinement module leverages the multiresolution decomposition property of wavelets to attenuate shortterm positional fluctuations and improve trajectory continuity. To the best of our knowledge, CDKF-Track is the first framework to jointly address detection-level fusion redundancy and learnable motion modeling in cooperative 3D MOT. Experimental results on the real-world V2V4Real dataset indicate that CDKF-Track achieves up to 27.99% improvements in tracking accuracy over state-of-the-art multi-agent MOT methods.

</details>


### [72] [Deploying Foundation Models for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25666)
*Vishnu Sashank Dorbala,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究基础模型（FM）在具身导航智能体部署中的两个关键限制：训练偏差导致新环境个性化不足，以及有限上下文长度影响长时任务表现。作者提出 TAP 与 MemCtrl 两种方法，并给出真实环境与多任务实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型用于具身导航时面临部署瓶颈：一是训练数据偏差使模型难以在未见环境中实现个性化目标查找；二是上下文长度有限，导致长时程、长指令任务成功率下降。论文旨在系统梳理现有 FM-based Embodied Navigation 文献，并针对上述限制提出可部署方案。

Method: 论文首先建立现有 FM-based 具身导航研究的分类体系（taxonomy），并指出其局限。针对训练偏差，提出 Transit-Aware Planning（TAP），通过从场景中挖掘人类习惯数据对 FM 进行 priming，以实现个性化目标查找。针对上下文限制，提出 MemCtrl，通过新颖的 memory head 增强实现主动记忆管理，从而在长时任务中更高效地利用上下文。
